/* File: js/api/mockData.js

const GAMEPLAY = { src: 'SIMPLIFIED_FOR_GAMEPLAY', verified: false };
const recalled = note => ({ src: 'UNVERIFIED_MEMORY', verified: false, note });

const makeOrbit = (n, dv, ecl, per, contact, res, rad) => ({ n, dv, ecl, per, contact, res, rad, ...GAMEPLAY });
const makeDestination = (id, ord, n, target, cap, life, orbits, extra = {}) => ({
  id, months: 24, n, dest: id, target, cap, ord, life, orbits, ...GAMEPLAY, ...extra
});

const CONTRACT_ORDER = { earth: 3, moon: 3.5, mars: 4 };
DATA.contracts.forEach(contract => { contract.ord = CONTRACT_ORDER[contract.id]; });

const addFactors = (table, values, extra) => {
  Object.entries(values).forEach(([dest, value]) => {
    table[dest] = { value, ...extra(dest) };
  });
};
addFactors(DATA.solarFluxFactor, { mercury: 3.0, venus: 1.9, jupiter: 0.037, saturn: 0.011, uranus: 0.0027, neptune: 0.0011, sun: 1 },
  () => recalled('Approximate sunlight relative to Earth at that distance (inverse-square).'));
addFactors(DATA.commsDistanceFactor, { mercury: 0.16, venus: 0.15, jupiter: 0.055, saturn: 0.03, uranus: 0.018, neptune: 0.012, sun: 0.5 },
  () => ({ ...GAMEPLAY, note: 'Deep Space Network downlink efficiency scaled for outer-planet gameplay.' }));
addFactors(DATA.transitDaysBase, { mercury: 110, venus: 150, jupiter: 950, saturn: 1850, uranus: 3200, neptune: 4000, sun: 90 },
  () => ({ unit: 'days', ...GAMEPLAY, note: 'Simplified one-way trip time with a chemical engine.' }));

  { dest: 'saturn', n: 'Cassini', yr: 1997, ins: ['i_cam', 'i_spec', 'i_radar', 'i_mag', 'i_therm'], oi: 1, verified: false, src: 'UNVERIFIED_MEMORY' },
  { dest: 'uranus', n: 'Voyager 2', yr: 1977, ins: ['i_cam', 'i_mag'], oi: 2, verified: false, src: 'UNVERIFIED_MEMORY' },
  { dest: 'neptune', n: 'Voyager 2', yr: 1977, ins: ['i_cam', 'i_mag'], oi: 2, verified: false, src: 'UNVERIFIED_MEMORY' },
  { dest: 'sun', n: 'Parker Solar Probe', yr: 2018, ins: ['i_mag', 'i_spec'], oi: 1, verified: false, src: 'UNVERIFIED_MEMORY' },
  { dest: 'sun', n: 'SOHO', yr: 1995, ins: ['i_cam', 'i_spec'], oi: 1, verified: false, src: 'UNVERIFIED_MEMORY' }
);

DATA.contracts.forEach(contract => {
  contract.thr = Math.round(contract.target * 0.6);
  contract.obj = OBJECTIVES_BY_MISSION[contract.id];
});

--- FILE: ./js/api/nasaService.js ---
/* File: js/api/nasaService.js
 * Owner: Person A (Ashmin)
 * AI-assisted: YES — Gemini, 2026-11-15. Reviewed by a human: YES Reviewd by Person C (Yochan)
 * Sources used: SPACEAPPS_CHALLENGE
 */
import { API_CONFIG } from '../config.js';
import { DATA } from './mockData.js';

const CACHE_KEY = 'apogeeNasaDonkiCache_v2';
const CACHE_TTL_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000;

function formatIsoDate(dateObj) {
  return dateObj.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

function readCachedWeather() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage[CACHE_KEY];
    if (!raw) return null;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (parsed && Date.now() - parsed.ts < CACHE_TTL_MS && parsed.spaceWeather) {
      return parsed.spaceWeather;
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
}

function writeCachedWeather(spaceWeather) {
  try {
    localStorage[CACHE_KEY] = JSON.stringify({ ts: Date.now(), spaceWeather });
  } catch {}
}

/**
 * Fetches live space weather (Solar Flares & Coronal Mass Ejections) from NASA's
 * public DONKI API over a 30-day window with caching, timeout, and offline fallback.
 */
export async function fetchMissionReferenceData() {
  const defaultSpaceWeather = {
    isLive: false,
    flareClass: 'M1.4 (Baseline)',
    flareCount: 3,
    cmeCount: 2,
    stormLevel: 'Moderate',
    radiationMultiplier: 1.0,
    summary: 'Solar wind & flare baseline (offline fallback — check internet connection for live NASA DONKI telemetry).',
    src: 'SIMPLIFIED_FOR_GAMEPLAY'
  };

  if (!API_CONFIG.enableLiveFetch) {
    return { sourceMode: 'Offline sample', data: DATA, spaceWeather: defaultSpaceWeather };
  }

  const cached = readCachedWeather();
  if (cached) {
    return { sourceMode: 'Live NASA data', data: DATA, spaceWeather: cached };
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), API_CONFIG.timeoutMs);

  try {
    const end = new Date();
    const start = new Date(end.getTime() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const rangeQuery = `&startDate=${formatIsoDate(start)}&endDate=${formatIsoDate(end)}`;

    const [flrRes, cmeRes] = await Promise.all([
      fetch(`${API_CONFIG.donkiFlareEndpoint}${rangeQuery}`, { signal: controller.signal }),
      fetch(`${API_CONFIG.donkiCmeEndpoint}${rangeQuery}`, { signal: controller.signal })
    ]);
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    if (!flrRes.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${flrRes.status}`);
    const flares = await flrRes.json();
    const cmes = cmeRes.ok ? await cmeRes.json() : [];

    const flareList = Array.isArray(flares) ? flares : [];
    const cmeList = Array.isArray(cmes) ? cmes : [];

    let peakClass = 'C1.0';
    let peakRank = 2;
    flareList.forEach(f => {
      const cls = String(f.classType || '').toUpperCase();
      const rank = cls.startsWith('X') ? 4 : cls.startsWith('M') ? 3 : cls.startsWith('C') ? 2 : 1;
      if (rank >= peakRank && cls) {
        peakRank = rank;
        peakClass = cls;
      }
    });

    const stormLevel = peakRank >= 4 ? `Severe (${peakClass})` : peakRank === 3 ? `Elevated (${peakClass})` : `Nominal (${peakClass})`;
    const radiationMultiplier = peakRank >= 4 ? 1.15 : peakRank === 3 ? 1.05 : 0.96;

    const spaceWeather = {
      isLive: true,
      flareClass: peakClass,
      flareCount: flareList.length,
      cmeCount: cmeList.length,
      stormLevel,
      radiationMultiplier,
      summary: `Live NASA DONKI (30-day telemetry): ${flareList.length} solar flares (peak ${peakClass}) & ${cmeList.length} CMEs tracked.`,
      src: 'NASA DONKI Space Weather API'
    };

    writeCachedWeather(spaceWeather);
    return { sourceMode: 'Live NASA data', data: DATA, spaceWeather };
  } catch {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    return { sourceMode: 'Offline sample', data: DATA, spaceWeather: defaultSpaceWeather };
  }
}

--- FILE: ./js/core/career.js ---
/* File: js/core/career.js
 * Owner: ported from the Apogee-temp-asmin feature branch (lib/career.js, v3.2)
 * AI-assisted: YES — Claude, 2026-11-14; Gemini, 2026-11-15. Reviewed by a human: YES Reviewd by Person C (Yochan)
 * Sources used: none (game design, not NASA data)
 */
import { DATA } from '../api/mockData.js';
import { CONTENT } from '../content.js';

export const HOME_IDS = ['earth', 'moon', 'mars'];

/* Engaging, paced unlock chain so players unlock new worlds steadily without tedious grinding */
export const GATES = [
  { id: 'venus', lv: 2, need: [['earth', 1], ['moon', 1]], title: 'Orbital Cadet' },
  { id: 'jupiter', lv: 4, need: [['mars', 1], ['venus', 1]], title: 'Deep-Space Navigator' },
  { id: 'saturn', lv: 6, need: [['jupiter', 1]], title: 'Ring Rider' },
  { id: 'uranus', lv: 8, need: [['saturn', 1, 1]], title: 'Ice Voyager' },
  { id: 'neptune', lv: 10, need: [['uranus', 1, 1]], title: 'Edge Walker' },
  { id: 'mercury', lv: 12, need: [['neptune', 1, 1]], title: 'Fire Walker' },
  { id: 'sun', lv: 14, need: [['mercury', 1, 1]], title: 'Master of the Solar System' }
];

const GRAND_TOUR = ['mercury', 'venus', 'earth', 'mars', 'jupiter', 'saturn', 'uranus', 'neptune'];
const STORAGE_KEY = 'apogeeCareer';
const emptyCareer = () => ({ arr: {}, good: {}, lost: 0, tot: 0, un: [], ach: {}, streak: 0, dev: false });

let career = emptyCareer();
try {
  const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage[STORAGE_KEY] || '{}');
  if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
    if ('god' in parsed && !('dev' in parsed)) {
      parsed.dev = !!parsed.god;
    }
    delete parsed.god;
    Object.assign(career, parsed);
  }
} catch {
  career = emptyCareer();
}

const saveCareer = () => {
  try {
    localStorage[STORAGE_KEY] = JSON.stringify(career);
  } catch {}
};

export const ACHIEVEMENTS = [
  { id: 'first', n: 'First Light', t: 'Make your first safe arrival.', test: x => x.ok },
  { id: 'star3', n: 'Flawless', t: 'Earn 3 stars on a mission.', test: x => x.st >= 3 },
  { id: 'thrift', n: 'Penny Pincher', t: 'Succeed while spending under 65% of the budget.', test: x => x.out === 'success' && x.d.cost <= x.C.cap * 0.65 },
  { id: 'home', n: 'Home Turf', t: 'Arrive safely at Earth, the Moon and Mars.', test: () => HOME_IDS.every(h => career.arr[h] > 0) },
  { id: 'nuke', n: 'Nuclear Option', t: 'Arrive safely using a nuclear thermal engine.', test: x => x.ok && x.d.E.patch === 'nuc' },
  { id: 'ion', n: 'Slow and Steady', t: 'Arrive safely using an ion engine.', test: x => x.ok && x.d.E.patch === 'ion' },
  { id: 'rtg', n: 'Atomic Battery', t: 'Arrive safely with a radioisotope generator aboard.', test: x => x.ok && x.rtg },
  { id: 'book', n: 'By the Book', t: 'Succeed under the NASA 35% margin policy.', test: x => x.out === 'success' && x.hard },
  { id: 'phoenix', n: 'Phoenix', t: 'Succeed right after losing a mission.', test: x => x.ok && x.wasLost },
  { id: 'learn', n: 'Learning the Hard Way', t: 'Lose 3 missions. Every engineer iterates.', test: () => career.lost >= 3 },
  { id: 'vet', n: 'Veteran', t: 'Make 8 safe arrivals.', test: () => career.tot >= 8 },
  { id: 'dir', n: 'Flight Director', t: 'Make 15 safe arrivals.', test: () => career.tot >= 15 },
  { id: 'tour', n: 'Grand Tour', t: 'Arrive safely at all eight planets.', test: () => GRAND_TOUR.every(h => career.arr[h] > 0) },
  { id: 'icarus', n: 'Icarus', t: 'Fly toward the Sun. It did not end well.', test: x => !!x.C.doom }
];

export const getCareer = () => career;
export const isDeveloperMode = () => !!career.dev;
export const isContractOpen = id => isDeveloperMode() || HOME_IDS.includes(id) || career.un.includes(id);

export function toggleDeveloperMode() {
  career.dev = !career.dev;
  saveCareer();
  return career.dev;
}

export const getContractSortKey = contract =>
  isContractOpen(contract.id) ? contract.ord : 100 + GATES.findIndex(g => g.id === contract.id);

export const getPlanetName = id => CONTENT.planetInfo[id].name.replace(/^the /, '');

const getTopGate = () => (isDeveloperMode() ? GATES[GATES.length - 1] : GATES.filter(g => career.un.includes(g.id)).pop());
export const getCareerLevel = () => (getTopGate() ? getTopGate().lv : 0);
export const getCareerRank = () => (isDeveloperMode() ? 'Developer Mode (All Maps)' : getTopGate() ? getTopGate().title : 'Launch Pad Rookie');
export const getNextGate = () => (isDeveloperMode() ? null : GATES.find(g => !career.un.includes(g.id)));
export const getCareerButtonLabel = () => `Career: Level ${getCareerLevel()} - ${getCareerRank()}`;

const countArrivals = (id, good) => (good ? career.good : career.arr)[id] || 0;

export const describeGateNeed = gate =>
  gate.need.map(([id, count, good]) => {
    const kind = good ? 'good' : 'safe';
    const plural = count > 1 ? 's' : '';
    const suffix = good ? ' (science above threshold)' : '';
    return `${count} ${kind} arrival${plural} at ${getPlanetName(id)}${suffix} (${Math.min(count, countArrivals(id, good))}/${count})`;
  }).join(', ');

export const getLockInfo = contract => {
  const gate = GATES.find(g => g.id === contract.id);
  return gate ? { level: gate.lv, need: describeGateNeed(gate) } : null;
};

export function recordMission({ out, C, d, st, rtg, sci, hard }) {
  const ok = out !== 'lost';
  const news = [];
  const wasLost = career.streak > 0;

  if (ok) {
    career.arr[C.id] = (career.arr[C.id] || 0) + 1;
    career.tot++;
    career.streak = 0;
    if ((sci || 0) >= C.thr) career.good[C.id] = (career.good[C.id] || 0) + 1;
  } else {
    career.lost++;
    career.streak++;
  }

  for (let changed = true; changed;) {
    changed = false;
    GATES.forEach(gate => {
      const met = gate.need.every(([id, count, good]) => countArrivals(id, good) >= count);
      if (!career.un.includes(gate.id) && met) {
        career.un.push(gate.id);
        news.push({ k: 'lv', lv: gate.lv, title: gate.title, planet: getPlanetName(gate.id) });
        changed = true;
      }
    });
  }

  const context = { ok, out, C, d, st, rtg, wasLost, hard: !!hard };
  ACHIEVEMENTS.forEach(a => {
    if (!career.ach[a.id] && a.test(context)) {
      career.ach[a.id] = 1;
      news.push({ k: 'ach', n: a.n, t: a.t });
    }
  });

  saveCareer();
  return news;
}

export function resetCareer() {
  career = emptyCareer();
  saveCareer();
}

export const getUngatedDestinations = () =>
  DATA.contracts.filter(c => !HOME_IDS.includes(c.id) && !GATES.some(g => g.id === c.id)).map(c => c.id);

--- FILE: ./js/core/engine.js ---
/* File: js/core/engine.js
 * Owner: Person A (Ashmin)
 * AI-assisted (update): Claude, 2026-11-15 - win/loss tightening (outcome bands, audio, demos). Reviewed by a human: NO.
 * AI-assisted: YES — Gemini, 2026-11-14; Gemini, 2026-11-15; Claude, 2026-11-14; Claude, 2026-11-15 (two manual burns). Reviewed by a human: Yes Reviewed by Person C (Yochan).
 * Sources used: NASA_CUBESAT101, JPL_CML
 */
import { DATA } from '../api/mockData.js';
import { CONTENT } from '../content.js';
import { getFeasibility } from './planets.js';
import { PART_DIMENSIONS, ATTACHMENT_NODES, MAX_PARTS, DEFAULT_SEED } from '../config.js';
import {
  appState,
  getNodeCategory,
  getDimensionKey,
  saveUndoSnapshot
} from './state.js';

export function createSeededRng(seed = DEFAULT_SEED) {
  let state = seed >>> 0;
  return function nextRandom() {
    state += 0x6d2b79f5;
    let t = Math.imul(state ^ (state >>> 15), 1 | state);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

/* ---- Pure Geometry & Node Graph Helpers ---- */

export const findPartByUid = uid => appState.mission.parts.find(p => p.u === uid);
export const getPartSize = part => PART_DIMENSIONS[getDimensionKey(part.id)];
export const isStackNode = nodeName => ['top', 'bottom', 'base'].includes(nodeName);

export function getNodeOffset(part, nodeName) {
  const [width, height] = getPartSize(part);
  const node = ATTACHMENT_NODES[getNodeCategory(part.id)].find(n => n[0] === nodeName);
  let x = (node[1] - 0.5) * width;
  let y = (node[2] - 0.5) * height;
  if (part.flip) x = -x;

  const rad = (part.rot * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cos = Math.cos(rad);
  const sin = Math.sin(rad);
  return [x * cos - y * sin, x * sin + y * cos];
}

export function getNodeWorldPosition(part, nodeName) {
  const [offsetX, offsetY] = getNodeOffset(part, nodeName);
  return [part.x + offsetX, part.y + offsetY];
}

export function getSubtreeUids(rootUid) {
  const uids = [rootUid];
  for (let i = 0; i < uids.length; i++) {
    appState.mission.parts.forEach(part => {
      if (part.par === uids[i]) uids.push(part.u);
    });
  }
  return uids;
}

export function getOccupiedNodes() {
  const occupied = new Set();
  appState.mission.parts.forEach(part => {
    if (part.par != null) {
      occupied.add(`${part.par}:${part.pn}`);
      occupied.add(`${part.u}:${part.cn}`);
    }
  });
  return occupied;
}

export function getConnectedPartUids() {
  const connected = new Set();
  appState.mission.parts.forEach(part => {
    let current = part;
    let depth = 0;
    while (current && current.par != null && depth++ < 80) {
      current = findPartByUid(current.par);
    }
    if (current && getNodeCategory(current.id) === 'bus') {
      connected.add(part.u);
    }
  });
  return connected;
}

/* ---- Spacecraft Assembly Operations ---- */

export function createPartInstance(partId, x, y) {
  const part = {
    u: appState.mission.nu++,
    id: partId,
    x,
    y,
    rot: 0,
    flip: 0,
    par: null
  };
  appState.mission.parts.push(part);
  return part;
}

export function movePartSubtree(rootUid, deltaX, deltaY) {
  getSubtreeUids(rootUid).forEach(uid => {
    const part = findPartByUid(uid);
    part.x += deltaX;
    part.y += deltaY;
  });
}

export function deletePartSubtree(rootUid) {
  const toDelete = new Set(getSubtreeUids(rootUid));
  appState.mission.parts = appState.mission.parts.filter(part => !toDelete.has(part.u));
}

export function attachParts(child, childNode, host, hostNode) {
  const hasChildren = appState.mission.parts.some(p => p.par === child.u);
  if (!isStackNode(childNode) && !isStackNode(hostNode) && !hasChildren) {
    child.rot = 0;
    child.flip = hostNode.startsWith('L') ? 1 : 0;
  }

  const [hostX, hostY] = getNodeWorldPosition(host, hostNode);
  const [childOffsetX, childOffsetY] = getNodeOffset(child, childNode);
  movePartSubtree(child.u, hostX - childOffsetX - child.x, hostY - childOffsetY - child.y);

  child.par = host.u;
  child.cn = childNode;
  child.pn = hostNode;
}

export function mirrorPartIfEnabled(childPart) {
  const host = findPartByUid(childPart.par);
  if (
    !appState.mission.mirror ||
    isStackNode(childPart.pn) ||
    host.rot !== 0 ||
    DATA.parts[childPart.id].g === 'sci' ||
    appState.mission.parts.length >= MAX_PARTS
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const oppositeSide = childPart.pn.startsWith('L') ? 'R' : 'L';
  const oppositeNode = oppositeSide + childPart.pn.slice(1);
  if (getOccupiedNodes().has(`${host.u}:${oppositeNode}`)) return;

  const twinPart = createPartInstance(childPart.id, 0, 0);
  attachParts(twinPart, childPart.cn, host, oppositeNode);
}

/**
 * Builds the starter spacecraft centered at (480, 495) of the 960x1100 world canvas,
 * omitting the communications antenna so players attach one before launching.
 */
export function buildStarterSpacecraft() {
  appState.mission.parts = [];
  appState.mission.nu = 1;

  const centerX = 480;
  const bus = createPartInstance('bus_rad', centerX, 395);
  const tank = createPartInstance('tank', centerX, 515);
  const engine = createPartInstance('chem', centerX, 625);

  attachParts(tank, 'top', bus, 'bottom');
  attachParts(engine, 'top', tank, 'bottom');

  const mountPart = (partId, host, hostNode) => {
    const part = createPartInstance(partId, 0, 0);
    const childNode = getNodeCategory(partId) === 'ant' ? 'base' : 'in';
    attachParts(part, childNode, host, hostNode);
    return part;
  };

  mirrorPartIfEnabled(mountPart('sol_s', bus, 'R1'));
  mountPart('i_cam', bus, 'R2');
  mountPart('bat', tank, 'R1');
}

export function buildFailingDemoSpacecraft() {
  // A design that passes every launch check but cannot be flown: a heavy radar craft with one tank is
  // short on delta-v for a low Mars orbit, so the flight (and the debrief) explains why it fails.
  appState.mission.ci = 2;
  appState.mission.oi = 0;
  appState.mission.L = 'medium';
  appState.mission.hard = 0;
  appState.mission.parts = [];
  appState.mission.nu = 1;

  const centerX = 480;
  const bus = createPartInstance('bus_red', centerX, 395);
  const tank = createPartInstance('tank', centerX, 515);
  const engine = createPartInstance('chem', centerX, 625);

  attachParts(tank, 'top', bus, 'bottom');
  attachParts(engine, 'top', tank, 'bottom');

  const mountPart = (partId, host, hostNode) => {
    const part = createPartInstance(partId, 0, 0);
    attachParts(part, 'in', host, hostNode);
    return part;
  };

  mountPart('sol_l', bus, 'L1');
  mountPart('sol_l', bus, 'R1');
  mountPart('i_radar', bus, 'R2');
  mountPart('bat', tank, 'R1');
  autoAttachPart('ant_hg');
}
