export function buildDeveloperPrebuilt(prebuiltId) {
  const spec = DEVELOPER_PREBUILTS.find(p => p.id === prebuiltId);
  if (!spec) return null;

  const contractIndex = DATA.contracts.findIndex(c => c.id === spec.contractId);
  if (contractIndex === -1) return null;

  appState.mission.ci = contractIndex;
  appState.mission.oi = spec.oi;
  appState.mission.L = spec.launcher;
  appState.mission.hard = spec.hard;
  appState.mission.locked = false;
  appState.mission.parts = [];
  appState.mission.nu = 1;

  const centerX = 480;
  const bus = createPartInstance(spec.bus, centerX, 340);
  const tanks = [];
  let prevHost = bus;

  for (let i = 0; i < spec.tanks; i++) {
    const tank = createPartInstance('tank', centerX, 460 + i * 120);
    attachParts(tank, 'top', prevHost, 'bottom');
    tanks.push(tank);
    prevHost = tank;
  }

  const engine = createPartInstance(spec.engine, centerX, 460 + spec.tanks * 120);
  attachParts(engine, 'top', prevHost, 'bottom');

  if (spec.ant) {
    const ant = createPartInstance(spec.ant, 0, 0);
    attachParts(ant, 'base', bus, 'top');
  }

  const resolveHost = ref => (ref === 'bus' ? bus : tanks[ref] || bus);

  (spec.pairs || []).forEach(([partId, hostRef, tier]) => {
    const host = resolveHost(hostRef);
    const rightPart = createPartInstance(partId, 0, 0);
    attachParts(rightPart, 'in', host, `R${tier}`);
    const leftPart = createPartInstance(partId, 0, 0);
    attachParts(leftPart, 'in', host, `L${tier}`);
  });

  (spec.singles || []).forEach(([partId, hostRef, nodeName]) => {
    const host = resolveHost(hostRef);
    const part = createPartInstance(partId, 0, 0);
    attachParts(part, 'in', host, nodeName);
  });

  return spec;
}

export function autoAttachPart(partId) {
  const category = getNodeCategory(partId);
  const connected = getConnectedPartUids();
  const hosts = appState.mission.parts
    .filter(p => connected.has(p.u))
    .sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y);
  const bus = appState.mission.parts.find(p => getNodeCategory(p.id) === 'bus');

  if (category === 'bus') {
    if (bus) {
      bus.id = partId;
    } else {
      saveUndoSnapshot();
      createPartInstance(partId, 480, 395);
    }
    return { ok: true };
  }

  if (!bus) {
    return { ok: false, message: 'Start with a spacecraft bus: it is the root everything bolts onto.' };
  }
  if (appState.mission.parts.length >= MAX_PARTS) {
    return { ok: false, message: `Part limit reached (${MAX_PARTS}).` };
  }

  const occupied = getOccupiedNodes();
  const isNodeFree = (host, node) => !occupied.has(`${host.u}:${node}`);
  const hasNode = (host, node) => ATTACHMENT_NODES[getNodeCategory(host.id)].some(n => n[0] === node);

  saveUndoSnapshot();

  if (category === 'eng') {
    const existingEngine = appState.mission.parts.find(p => getNodeCategory(p.id) === 'eng');
    if (existingEngine) {
      existingEngine.id = partId;
      if (existingEngine.par != null) {
        attachParts(existingEngine, 'top', findPartByUid(existingEngine.par), existingEngine.pn);
      }
      return { ok: true };
    }
    const bottomHost = [...hosts].reverse().find(h => hasNode(h, 'bottom') && isNodeFree(h, 'bottom'));
    if (!bottomHost) return { ok: false, message: 'Nothing to bolt an engine under.' };
    attachParts(createPartInstance(partId, 0, 0), 'top', bottomHost, 'bottom');
    return { ok: true };
  }

  if (category === 'tank') {
    const lowestStack = [...hosts].reverse().find(h => ['bus', 'tank'].includes(getNodeCategory(h.id)));
    const attachedBottom = appState.mission.parts.find(p => p.par === lowestStack.u && p.pn === 'bottom');
    const newTank = createPartInstance('tank', 0, 0);

    if (attachedBottom) {
      attachedBottom.par = null;
      attachParts(newTank, 'top', lowestStack, 'bottom');
      attachParts(attachedBottom, attachedBottom.cn, newTank, 'bottom');
    } else {
      attachParts(newTank, 'top', lowestStack, 'bottom');
    }
    return { ok: true };
  }

  if (category === 'ant') {
    const topHost = hosts.find(h => ['bus', 'tank'].includes(getNodeCategory(h.id)) && isNodeFree(h, 'top'));
    if (!topHost) return { ok: false, message: 'No free mounting point on top. Move something first.' };
    attachParts(createPartInstance(partId, 0, 0), 'base', topHost, 'top');
    return { ok: true };
  }

  for (const host of hosts) {
    for (const sideNode of ['R1', 'L1', 'R2', 'L2']) {
      if (hasNode(host, sideNode) && isNodeFree(host, sideNode)) {
        const part = createPartInstance(partId, 0, 0);
        attachParts(part, 'in', host, sideNode);
        mirrorPartIfEnabled(part);
        return { ok: true };
      }
    }
  }

  return { ok: false, message: 'No free mounting point. Drag a part off the ship, or add a tank for more mounts.' };
}

/* ---- Pure Physics & Engineering Evaluation ---- */

export function calculateTelemetry(missionInput = appState.mission) {
  const threshold = missionInput.hard ? DATA.marginPolicies.hard.v : DATA.marginPolicies.easy.v;
  appState.marginThreshold = threshold;

  const contract = DATA.contracts[missionInput.ci];
  const orbit = contract.orbits[missionInput.oi];
  const launcher = DATA.launchers[missionInput.L];
  const tankSpec = DATA.tank;
  const weather = appState.spaceWeather;

  const connectedUids = getConnectedPartUids();
  const connectedParts = missionInput.parts.filter(p => connectedUids.has(p.u));

  const busPart = connectedParts.find(p => getNodeCategory(p.id) === 'bus');
  const engineParts = connectedParts.filter(p => getNodeCategory(p.id) === 'eng');

  const busSpec = DATA.parts[busPart ? busPart.id : 'bus_std'];
  const engineSpec = DATA.engines[engineParts[0] ? engineParts[0].id : 'chem'];

  const telemetry = {
    C: contract,
    O: orbit,
    B: busSpec,
    E: engineSpec,
    L: launcher,
    dry: busSpec.m + engineParts.length * engineSpec.m,
    load: busSpec.pw,
    gen: 0,
    rg: 0,
    wh: 0,
    mb: 0,
    gb: DATA.constants.baseStorageGb.value,
    rec: 0,
    data: 0,
    sci: 0,
    tk: 0,
    cost: busSpec.c + engineParts.length * engineSpec.c + launcher.c
  };

  let propellantMass = 0;
  let weightedX = 0;
  let weightedY = 0;
  let totalMass = 0;
  const seenInstruments = {};

  connectedParts.forEach(part => {
    let partMass = 0;
    const category = getNodeCategory(part.id);

    if (part.id === 'tank') {
      telemetry.tk++;
      telemetry.dry += tankSpec.dry;
      telemetry.cost += tankSpec.c;
      propellantMass += tankSpec.prop;
      partMass = tankSpec.dry + tankSpec.prop;
    } else if (category === 'eng') {
      partMass = engineSpec.m;
    } else if (category === 'bus') {
      partMass = busSpec.m;
    } else {
      const spec = DATA.parts[part.id];
      partMass = spec.m;
      telemetry.dry += spec.m;
      telemetry.cost += spec.c;
      telemetry.load += spec.pw || 0;
      if (spec.rtg) telemetry.rg += spec.gen || 0;
      else telemetry.gen += spec.gen || 0;
      telemetry.wh += spec.wh || 0;
      telemetry.mb = Math.max(telemetry.mb, spec.mb || 0);

      if (spec.gb) {
        telemetry.gb += spec.gb;
        telemetry.rec++;
      }
      telemetry.data += spec.data || 0;
      if (spec.sci) {
        telemetry.sci += spec.sci * (seenInstruments[part.id] ? 0.25 : 1);
        seenInstruments[part.id] = true;
      }
    }

    totalMass += partMass;
    weightedX += partMass * part.x;
    weightedY += partMass * part.y;
  });

  telemetry.comx = totalMass ? weightedX / totalMass : 0;
  telemetry.comy = totalMass ? weightedY / totalMass : 0;
  telemetry.engx = engineParts[0] ? engineParts[0].x : telemetry.comx;
  telemetry.off = engineParts.length ? Math.abs(telemetry.comx - telemetry.engx) : 0;
  telemetry.box = connectedParts.length
    ? [Math.min(...connectedParts.map(p => p.y)) - 30, Math.max(...connectedParts.map(p => p.y)) + 80]
    : [0, 0];

  telemetry.m0 = telemetry.dry + propellantMass;
  telemetry.dv = engineSpec.isp * DATA.constants.g0.value * Math.log(telemetry.m0 / telemetry.dry);
  telemetry.sun = telemetry.gen * DATA.solarFluxFactor[contract.dest].value * (1 - orbit.ecl) + telemetry.rg;
  telemetry.ecH = orbit.ecl * orbit.per;
  telemetry.down = telemetry.mb * DATA.commsDistanceFactor[contract.dest].value * orbit.contact * 86.4;
  telemetry.days = Math.max(
    DATA.transitDaysBase[contract.dest].value * engineSpec.tf,
    engineSpec.dvDay ? (orbit.dv * 1000) / engineSpec.dvDay : 0
  );

  const radWear = orbit.rad && !busSpec.hard
    ? Math.max(0.5, 0.65 / (weather?.radiationMultiplier || 1))
    : orbit.rad > 1
      ? 0.75
      : 1;
  telemetry.baseLife = contract.life || DATA.constants.designLifeMonths.value;
  telemetry.life = telemetry.baseLife * radWear - telemetry.days / 30;

  const buildCheckRow = (name, category, used, capacity, unit, src, verified) => {
    const margin = used <= 0 ? 1 : capacity > 0 ? (capacity - used) / used : -1;
    const lesson = CONTENT.lessons[name];
    return {
      id: name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_'),
      category,
      n: name,
      use: used,
      cap: capacity,
      u: unit,
      mg: margin,
      pass: margin >= 0,
      severity: margin < 0 ? 'critical' : margin < threshold ? 'warning' : 'info',
      why: lesson.why,
      fix: lesson.fix,
      src,
      verified
    };
  };

  const requiredContactHours = telemetry.data
    ? (telemetry.mb ? telemetry.data / (telemetry.mb * DATA.commsDistanceFactor[contract.dest].value * 3.6) : 99)
    : 0;

  telemetry.rows = [
    buildCheckRow('Mass', 'mass', telemetry.m0, launcher.cap, 'kg', launcher.src, launcher.verified),
    buildCheckRow('Delta-v', 'launch', orbit.dv * 1000, telemetry.dv, 'm/s', orbit.src, orbit.verified),
    buildCheckRow('Power', 'power', telemetry.load, telemetry.sun, 'W', DATA.solarFluxFactor[contract.dest].src, false),
    buildCheckRow('Battery', 'power', telemetry.load * telemetry.ecH, telemetry.wh * DATA.constants.batteryDepthOfDischarge.value, 'Wh', DATA.constants.batteryDepthOfDischarge.src, false),
    buildCheckRow('Budget', 'budget', telemetry.cost, contract.cap, '$M', contract.src, contract.verified),
    buildCheckRow('Data storage', 'data', telemetry.data * (1 - orbit.contact), telemetry.gb, 'Gb', DATA.constants.baseStorageGb.src, false),
    buildCheckRow('Comms', 'comms', requiredContactHours, orbit.contact * 24, 'h/day', DATA.commsDistanceFactor[contract.dest].src, false),
    buildCheckRow('Time', 'time', contract.months, telemetry.life, 'months', weather?.isLive ? weather.src : DATA.constants.designLifeMonths.src, weather?.isLive || false)
  ];

  if (engineSpec.pw) {
    telemetry.rows.push(
      buildCheckRow('Ion power', 'power', engineSpec.pw, telemetry.gen + telemetry.rg, 'W', engineSpec.src, engineSpec.verified)
    );
  }

  const getRow = name => telemetry.rows.find(r => r.n === name);
  telemetry.g = getRow;

  const flightModel = DATA.flightModel;
  const floatingCount = missionInput.parts.length - connectedParts.length;
  const isEngineValid = busPart && engineParts.length === 1 && engineParts[0].rot === 0 && engineParts[0].y > busPart.y;

  telemetry.checks = [
    {
      t: 'A spacecraft bus is the root of the ship',
      st: busPart ? 2 : 0,
      why: 'Everything must bolt onto the bus.',
      fix: 'Add a spacecraft bus from the Structure list.'
    },
    {
      t: 'Exactly one engine, nozzle down, below the bus',
      st: isEngineValid ? 2 : 0,
      why: 'Rockets must apply thrust axially from the aft stack.',
      fix: 'Attach one engine facing down beneath your fuel tanks.'
    },
    {
      t: 'Every part is attached',
      st: floatingCount ? 0 : 2,
      why: `${floatingCount} part(s) are unattached (highlighted red).`,
      fix: 'Drag unattached parts onto a glowing attachment node or delete them.'
    },
    {
      t: 'Balanced: thrust line through the center of mass',
      st: telemetry.off <= flightModel.balanceGoodPx ? 2 : telemetry.off <= flightModel.balanceLimitPx ? 1 : 0,
      why: `Center of mass is ${telemetry.off.toFixed(0)} px off the thrust line (tumbles above ${flightModel.balanceLimitPx} px).`,
      fix: 'Mirror heavy parts symmetrically left and right of the centerline.'
    }
  ];

  const dataReturnRatio = telemetry.data
    ? Math.min(1, telemetry.down / telemetry.data) * (getRow('Data storage').mg < 0 ? telemetry.gb / (telemetry.data * (1 - orbit.contact)) : 1)
    : 0;

  telemetry.ret = dataReturnRatio;
  telemetry.sciF =
    telemetry.sci *
    orbit.res *
    dataReturnRatio *
    (Math.max(0, telemetry.life) / telemetry.baseLife) *
    (getRow('Power').mg < 0 ? 0.5 : 1) *
    (getRow('Battery').mg < 0 ? 0.6 : 1);

  const hasHealthyMargin = name => getRow(name).mg >= threshold;
  const isPositiveMargin = name => getRow(name).mg >= 0;

  telemetry.sign = {
    Director: isPositiveMargin('Mass') && isPositiveMargin('Budget') && isPositiveMargin('Time'),
    Engineer:
      hasHealthyMargin('Delta-v') &&
      hasHealthyMargin('Power') &&
      hasHealthyMargin('Battery') &&
      (!engineSpec.pw || hasHealthyMargin('Ion power')) &&
      telemetry.checks[3].st === 2,
    Science: telemetry.sciF >= contract.target,
    Comms: hasHealthyMargin('Comms') && hasHealthyMargin('Data storage') && telemetry.data > 0
  };
  telemetry.signed = Object.values(telemetry.sign).every(Boolean);

  telemetry.revItems = [
    ...telemetry.rows.map(r => [r.n, r.mg < 0 ? 0 : r.mg < threshold ? 1 : 2]),
    ...telemetry.checks.map(c => [c.t, c.st]),
    ['Science vs threshold goal', telemetry.sciF >= contract.target ? 2 : telemetry.sciF >= contract.thr ? 1 : 0]
  ];

  const itemCount = telemetry.revItems.length;
  const countStatus = code => telemetry.revItems.filter(item => item[1] === code).length;
  telemetry.rev = {
    n: itemCount,
    g: countStatus(2),
    y: countStatus(1),
    r: countStatus(0),
    pct: countStatus(2) / itemCount,
    pass: countStatus(2) / itemCount >= DATA.reviewThreshold.v
  };

  const marginPenalty = telemetry.rows.reduce(
    (sum, r) => (r.mg >= 0 && r.mg < threshold ? sum + (threshold - r.mg) * flightModel.amberPenaltyFactor : sum),
    0
  );
  const balancePenalty = Math.min(
    flightModel.balancePenaltyAtLimit,
    (telemetry.off / flightModel.balanceLimitPx) * flightModel.balancePenaltyAtLimit
  );
  const signOffBonus = telemetry.signed ? flightModel.signOffBonus : 0;
  const reviewPenalty = telemetry.rev.pass ? 0 : flightModel.reviewFailPenalty;
  const baseReliability = launcher.rel * busSpec.rel;
  const adjustment = -marginPenalty - balancePenalty + signOffBonus - reviewPenalty;

  telemetry.reliabilityParts = { launcher: launcher.rel, bus: busSpec.rel, adjustment };
  telemetry.reliability = Math.max(
    flightModel.reliabilityMin,
    Math.min(flightModel.reliabilityMax, baseReliability + adjustment)
  );
  telemetry.bad = telemetry.checks.slice(0, 3).find(c => c.st === 0);

  return telemetry;
}

/* ---- Deterministic Flight Resolution & Present-Day Events ---- */

export function fillTemplate(template, values = {}) {
  return template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (match, key) => {
    const value = values[key];
    if (typeof value !== 'number') return value === undefined ? match : String(value);
    return key.endsWith('Pct') ? String(Math.round(value * 100)) : Math.round(value).toLocaleString();
  });
}

function doesEventTrigger(event, telemetry) {
  const { trigger } = event;
  if (trigger.dest !== undefined) return telemetry.C.dest === trigger.dest;
  if (trigger.minTransitDays !== undefined) return telemetry.days >= trigger.minTransitDays;
  const value = telemetry.O[trigger.orbitField];
  if (trigger.gte !== undefined && value < trigger.gte) return false;
  if (trigger.lte !== undefined && value > trigger.lte) return false;
  return true;
}

function isEventImmune(event, telemetry) {
  if (event.imm === 'hard') return !!telemetry.B.hard;
  if (event.imm === 'rec') return telemetry.rec > 0;
  return !!telemetry.B.red;
}

export function getTriggeredEvents(telemetry) {
  const weather = appState.spaceWeather;
  return DATA.events
    .filter(event => doesEventTrigger(event, telemetry))
    .map(event => {
      const immune = isEventImmune(event, telemetry);
      let factor = event.scienceFactor;
      if (!immune && event.id === 'solar_flare' && weather?.radiationMultiplier > 1) {
        factor = Math.max(0.65, +(event.scienceFactor / weather.radiationMultiplier).toFixed(2));
      }
      return {
        id: event.id,
        immune,
        scienceFactor: immune ? 1 : factor
      };
    });
}

export function scoreFlight(telemetry, outcome, science) {
  const weights = DATA.scoringWeights;
  const contract = telemetry.C;

  let stars = outcome === 'lost' ? 0 : 1;
  if (science >= contract.target) stars = 2;
  if (stars === 2 && telemetry.signed && telemetry.cost <= contract.cap * weights.threeStarBudgetFraction) stars = 3;

  const score = outcome === 'lost'
    ? 0
    : Math.round(
        (science * weights.scienceMultiplier +
          (contract.cap - telemetry.cost) * weights.budgetSavingsMultiplier +
          stars * weights.starBonus) *
          (telemetry.signed ? weights.fullSignOffMultiplier : 1) *
          telemetry.reliability
      );

  return { stars, score };
}

/**
 * One place that decides how a flight ends, so the flight, the re-score after mid-flight decisions and the
 * predicted flight can never disagree.
 *   success = science reaches the contract baseline (target)
 *   partial = science reaches the minimum (threshold) but not the baseline
 *   lost    = no science, or less than the minimum
 * The minimum is the contract threshold times DATA.flightModel.failBelowThresholdFactor.
 */
export function classifyOutcome(science, contract) {
  const minimum = contract.thr * DATA.flightModel.failBelowThresholdFactor;
  if (science >= contract.target) return 'success';
  if (science > 0 && science >= minimum) return 'partial';
  return 'lost';
}

const clamp01 = value => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(value) || 0));

const BURN_ORDER = ['departure', 'insertion'];

function getBurnQuality(burns, kind) {
  return burns && burns[kind] ? clamp01(burns[kind].quality) : null;
}

export function scoreBurn(fill, kind = 'departure') {
  const model = DATA.flightModel;
  const spec = model.burns[kind];
  const clamped = clamp01(fill);
  if (!spec) return { kind, fill: clamped, quality: 0, bonus: 0 };
  const quality = clamp01(1 - Math.abs(clamped - spec.target) / spec.zoneHalfWidth);
  return { kind, fill: clamped, quality, bonus: quality * spec.weight * model.burnBonusMax };
}

export function resolveFlight(telemetry, burns = null) {
  const model = DATA.flightModel;
  const contract = telemetry.C;
  const steps = [];

  const finish = (outcome, science, events, reasons, arrived = false) => ({
    outcome,
    arrived, // true once the craft is safely in orbit, even if the science was too low to count
    science,
    events,
    reasons,
    steps,
    reliability: telemetry.reliability,
    ...scoreFlight(telemetry, outcome, science)
  });

  const lose = (key, values, reasonKey) => {
    steps.push({ key, ok: false, fatal: true, values });
    const reason = CONTENT.flightFailures[reasonKey];
    return finish('lost', 0, [], [`${reason.why} Fix: ${reason.fix}`]);
  };

  const deltaV = telemetry.g('Delta-v');

  steps.push({
    key: 'liftoff',
    ok: true,
    values: { launcher: telemetry.L.n, massKg: telemetry.m0, reliabilityPct: telemetry.reliability }
  });

  if (telemetry.off > model.balanceLimitPx) {
    return lose('tumble', { offPx: telemetry.off, limitPx: model.balanceLimitPx }, 'tumble');
  }
  steps.push({ key: 'ascent', ok: true, values: { reliabilityPct: telemetry.reliability } });

  const feasibility = getFeasibility(contract);
  steps.push({
    key: 'feasibility',
    ok: true,
    values: {
      name: contract.n,
      cls: feasibility.cls,
      label: feasibility.lab,
      notes: feasibility.notes.join('; '),
      haveMs: telemetry.dv,
      needMs: deltaV.use,
      haveW: telemetry.sun,
      needW: telemetry.load,
      days: telemetry.days
    }
  });

  if (contract.doom) {
    CONTENT.sunDive.lines.forEach(([text, cls]) => {
      steps.push({ key: 'sun_dive', ok: cls !== 'bad', values: { text } });
    });
    steps.push({ key: 'sun_boom', ok: false, fatal: true, values: { text: CONTENT.sunDive.boom } });
    const sunReasons = CONTENT.sunDive.why.map((line, index) =>
      index === 0 ? `${line} Your craft had ${Math.round(telemetry.dv).toLocaleString()} m/s.` : line
    );
    return finish('lost', 0, [], sunReasons);
  }

  steps.push({ key: 'transfer', ok: deltaV.mg >= 0, values: { needMs: deltaV.use, haveMs: telemetry.dv } });
  if (deltaV.mg < 0) {
    return lose('deltav_short', { shortMs: deltaV.use - telemetry.dv }, 'deltav');
  }
  if (telemetry.E.pw && telemetry.g('Ion power').mg < 0) {
    return lose('ion_short', { needW: telemetry.E.pw }, 'ion');
  }

  const burnShare = kind => {
    const quality = getBurnQuality(burns, kind);
    return quality === null ? 0 : quality * model.burns[kind].weight * model.burnBonusMax;
  };
  const burnBonus = Math.min(model.burnBonusMax, BURN_ORDER.reduce((total, kind) => total + burnShare(kind), 0));
  const pushBurnStep = kind => {
    const quality = getBurnQuality(burns, kind);
    if (quality === null) return;
    steps.push({
      key: quality > 0 ? `burn_${kind}` : `burn_${kind}_miss`,
      ok: true,
      values: { precisionPct: quality, bonusPct: burnShare(kind) }
    });
  };

  pushBurnStep('departure');
  steps.push({ key: 'arrival', ok: true, values: { days: telemetry.days, orbit: telemetry.O.n.toLowerCase() } });
  pushBurnStep('insertion');
  steps.push({ key: 'briefing', ok: true, values: { contractId: contract.id } });

  const events = getTriggeredEvents(telemetry);
  events.forEach(event => {
    steps.push({
      key: 'event',
      ok: event.immune,
      values: {
        eventId: event.id,
        scienceFactor: event.scienceFactor,
        flareClass: appState.spaceWeather?.flareClass || 'M1.4'
      }
    });
  });

  const science = events.reduce((total, event) => total * event.scienceFactor, telemetry.sciF) * (1 + burnBonus);
  steps.push({
    key: 'operations',
    ok: true,
    values: { returnedPct: telemetry.ret, science, target: contract.target }
  });

  const outcome = classifyOutcome(science, contract);
  const minimumScience = contract.thr * model.failBelowThresholdFactor;

  const reasons = [];
  if (outcome === 'lost') {
    reasons.push(fillTemplate(CONTENT.flightFailures.belowMinimum, { sciPts: science, minPts: minimumScience }));
  }
  reasons.push(...telemetry.rows
    .filter(r => r.mg < appState.marginThreshold)
    .map(r => `${r.n}: ${r.why} Fix: ${r.fix} [${r.src}]`));

  if (telemetry.ret < 1 && telemetry.data) {
    reasons.push(fillTemplate(CONTENT.flightFailures.dataReturn, { returnedPct: telemetry.ret }));
  }

  events.filter(event => !event.immune).forEach(event => {
    const text = CONTENT.eventText[event.id];
    reasons.push(`${text.n}: ${text.hitT} ${fillTemplate(CONTENT.flightFailures.eventLoss, { lossPct: 1 - event.scienceFactor })} Fix: ${text.fix}`);
  });

  if (BURN_ORDER.every(kind => getBurnQuality(burns, kind) === null)) {
    reasons.push(fillTemplate(CONTENT.flightFailures.burnAuto, { maxBonusPct: model.burnBonusMax }));
  } else {
    BURN_ORDER.forEach(kind => {
      const quality = getBurnQuality(burns, kind);
      const name = CONTENT.burn[kind].name;
      if (quality === null) {
        reasons.push(fillTemplate(CONTENT.flightFailures.burnSkipped, { name }));
      } else if (quality > 0) {
        reasons.push(fillTemplate(CONTENT.flightFailures.burn, { name, precisionPct: quality, bonusPct: burnShare(kind) }));
      } else {
        reasons.push(fillTemplate(CONTENT.flightFailures.burnMiss, { name }));
      }
    });
  }

  const reliabilityLine = fillTemplate(CONTENT.flightFailures.reliability, {
    reliabilityPct: telemetry.reliability,
    launcherPct: telemetry.reliabilityParts.launcher,
    busPct: telemetry.reliabilityParts.bus,
    adjustmentPct: telemetry.reliabilityParts.adjustment
  });
  reasons.push(reliabilityLine + (telemetry.reliabilityParts.adjustment < 0 ? CONTENT.flightFailures.reliabilityFix : ''));

  return finish(outcome, science, events, reasons, true);
}

--- FILE: ./js/core/planets.js ---
/* File: js/core/planets.js
 * Owner: ported from the Apogee-temp-asmin feature branch (lib/planets.js, v3.2)
 * AI-assisted: YES — Claude, 2026-11-14. Reviewed by a human: Yes Reviewed by Person C (Yochan).
 * Sources used: none (game design; planet facts are UNVERIFIED_MEMORY, see content.js)
 */
import { DATA } from '../api/mockData.js';
import { CONTENT } from '../content.js';

/**
 * Best delta-v (km/s) the game lets you build: super-heavy launcher, hardened bus, as many tanks as fit.
 * Used only to label how hard a destination is before the player designs anything.
 */
const BEST_BUILDABLE_DV = (() => {
  const tank = DATA.tank;
  const bus = DATA.parts.bus_rad;
  const result = {};

  for (const key of ['chem', 'nuclear']) {
    const engine = DATA.engines[key];
    let best = 0;
    for (let tanks = 1; tanks <= 8; tanks++) {
      const dry = bus.m + engine.m + tanks * tank.dry + 250;
      const wet = dry + tanks * tank.prop;
      if (wet <= DATA.launchers.super.cap) {
        best = Math.max(best, engine.isp * DATA.constants.g0.value * Math.log(wet / dry));
      }
    }
    result[key] = best / 1000;
  }
  return result;
})();

/**
 * Rates a destination before design: Feasible, Hard, Extreme, Beyond current engines, or IMPOSSIBLE.
 * Returns { lab, cls, notes[], dv } where cls is a colour class (g / y / r).
 */
export function getFeasibility(contract) {
  if (contract.doom) {
    return { lab: 'IMPOSSIBLE', cls: 'r', notes: ['no spacecraft can survive it; try it and see why'], dv: Infinity };
  }

  const cheapestDv = Math.min(...contract.orbits.map(o => o.dv));
  const chemical = BEST_BUILDABLE_DV.chem;
  const nuclear = BEST_BUILDABLE_DV.nuclear;
  const sunlight = DATA.solarFluxFactor[contract.dest].value;
  const commsFactor = DATA.commsDistanceFactor[contract.dest].value;
  const tripDays = DATA.transitDaysBase[contract.dest].value;

  let lab;
  let cls;
  if (cheapestDv <= chemical * 0.65) { lab = 'Feasible'; cls = 'g'; }
  else if (cheapestDv <= chemical * 0.95) { lab = 'Hard: giant chemical rocket'; cls = 'y'; }
  else if (cheapestDv <= nuclear * 0.95) { lab = 'Extreme: needs nuclear engine'; cls = 'y'; }
  else { lab = 'Beyond current engines'; cls = 'r'; }

  const notes = [
    `needs at least ${cheapestDv.toFixed(1)} km/s of delta-v`,
    `trip about ${tripDays < 120 ? `${tripDays} days` : `${(tripDays / 365).toFixed(1)} years`} (chemical engine)`
  ];
  if (sunlight < 0.2) notes.push(`sunlight only ${(sunlight * 100).toFixed(1)}% of Earth’s: bring radioisotope generators`);
  else if (sunlight > 2.5) notes.push(`sunlight ${sunlight}x Earth’s: heat is the enemy`);
  if (commsFactor < 0.05) notes.push(`weak signal: only ${(commsFactor * 100).toFixed(1)}% of the data rate, choose light instruments`);

  return { lab, cls, notes, dv: cheapestDv };
}

/** Destination briefing (gravity, climate, moons...) as an HTML snippet. `welcome` adds a heading line. */
export function buildPlanetBriefing(contractId, welcome = false) {
  const info = CONTENT.planetInfo[contractId];
  if (!info) return '';
  const felt = Math.round(70 * info.gx);
  return `<span class="pin">${welcome ? `<b>Welcome to ${info.name}</b><br>` : ''}` +
    `<b>Gravity:</b> ${info.grav} m/s² (${info.gx}x Earth). A 70 kg person would feel like ${felt} kg${info.gnote ? ` (${info.gnote})` : ''}.<br>` +
    `<b>Environment:</b> ${info.env}<br>` +
    `<b>Temperature:</b> ${info.temp}<br>` +
    `<b>Atmosphere:</b> ${info.atm}<br>` +
    `<b>Day and year:</b> ${info.day}<br>` +
    `<b>Moons and rings:</b> ${info.moons}<br>` +
    `<b>Unique:</b> ${info.uniq}</span>`;
}


--- FILE: ./js/core/state.js ---
/* File: js/core/state.js
 * Owner: Person A (Ashmin)
 * AI-assisted (update): Claude, 2026-11-15 - AudioEngine.pauseAll/resumeAll for the flight pause. Reviewed by a human: NO.
 * AI-assisted (update): Claude, 2026-11-15 - win/loss tightening (outcome bands, audio, demos). Reviewed by a human: NO.
 * AI-assisted: YES — Gemini, 2026-11-15. Reviewed by a human: Yes Reviewed by Person C (Yochan).
 * Sources used: none
 */
import { DATA } from '../api/mockData.js';
import { CONTENT } from '../content.js';
import { MAX_HISTORY, DEFAULT_SEED } from '../config.js';

const MISSION_STORAGE_KEY = 'apogeeMissionState';
const SAVED_BUILDS_KEY = 'apogeeSavedBuilds';
const VOLUME_STORAGE_KEY = 'apogeeVolume';
const MAX_SAVED_BUILDS = 12;

export function createInitialMission(contractIndex = 1) {
  return {
    stage: 1,
    ci: contractIndex,
    oi: 0,
    L: 'medium',
    parts: [],
    nu: 1,
    mirror: true,
    locked: false,
    hard: 0,
    seed: DEFAULT_SEED
  };
}

export const appState = {
  mission: createInitialMission(1),
  marginThreshold: 0.1,
  candidates: [],
  savedBuilds: [],
  previousErrorCount: 0,
  highScores: {},
  isFlying: false,
  selectedPartUid: null,
  canvasZoom: 0.9,
  undoHistory: [],
  activeDrag: null,
  dataSourceLabel: 'Offline sample',
  spaceWeather: {
    isLive: false,
    flareClass: 'M1.4 (Baseline)',
    flareCount: 3,
    cmeCount: 2,
    stormLevel: 'Moderate',
    radiationMultiplier: 1.0,
    summary: 'Solar wind & flare baseline (offline fallback — check internet connection for live NASA DONKI telemetry).',
    src: 'SIMPLIFIED_FOR_GAMEPLAY'
  }
};

try {
  appState.highScores = JSON.parse(localStorage.tm || '{}');
} catch {
  appState.highScores = {};
}

try {
  const rawBuilds = JSON.parse(localStorage[SAVED_BUILDS_KEY] || '[]');
  if (Array.isArray(rawBuilds)) appState.savedBuilds = rawBuilds;
} catch {
  appState.savedBuilds = [];
}

function persistSavedBuilds() {
  try {
    localStorage[SAVED_BUILDS_KEY] = JSON.stringify(appState.savedBuilds);
  } catch {}
}

export function saveNamedBuild(customName = '') {
  const { parts, nu, L, oi, ci, hard, seed, d } = appState.mission;
  if (!parts.length) return { ok: false, message: 'Add at least one part before saving a build.' };

  const contractName = DATA.contracts[ci]?.n || 'Mission';
  const name = customName.trim() || `${contractName} Build #${appState.savedBuilds.length + 1}`;
  const entry = {
    id: `build_${Date.now()}`,
    name,
    savedAt: new Date().toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    mission: { parts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(parts)), nu, L, oi, ci, hard, seed },
    summary: {
      contract: contractName,
      orbit: DATA.contracts[ci]?.orbits[oi]?.n || '',
      launcher: DATA.launchers[L]?.n || L,
      massKg: d ? Math.round(d.m0) : 0,
      dvMs: d ? Math.round(d.dv) : 0,
      costM: d ? Math.round(d.cost) : 0,
      partCount: parts.length
    }
  };

  appState.savedBuilds.unshift(entry);
  if (appState.savedBuilds.length > MAX_SAVED_BUILDS) {
    appState.savedBuilds.length = MAX_SAVED_BUILDS;
  }
  persistSavedBuilds();
  return { ok: true, entry };
}

export function deleteSavedBuild(buildId) {
  appState.savedBuilds = appState.savedBuilds.filter(b => b.id !== buildId);
  persistSavedBuilds();
}

export function saveMissionProgress() {
  try {
    const { d, res, ...serializable } = appState.mission;
    localStorage[MISSION_STORAGE_KEY] = JSON.stringify({
      mission: serializable,
      candidates: appState.candidates
    });
  } catch {}
}

export function loadSavedMissionProgress() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage[MISSION_STORAGE_KEY];
    if (!raw) return false;
    const saved = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (saved && saved.mission && Array.isArray(saved.mission.parts) && saved.mission.parts.length > 0) {
      const validParts = saved.mission.parts.every(
        p => p.id === 'tank' || DATA.parts[p.id] || DATA.engines[p.id]
      );
      if (validParts && DATA.contracts[saved.mission.ci] && DATA.launchers[saved.mission.L]) {
        appState.mission = { ...createInitialMission(saved.mission.ci), ...saved.mission, stage: 1 };
        if (Array.isArray(saved.candidates)) {
          appState.candidates = saved.candidates.slice(0, 3);
        }
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  return false;
}

export function getNodeCategory(partId) {
  if (partId === 'tank') return 'tank';
  if (DATA.engines[partId]) return 'eng';
  if (DATA.parts[partId]?.g === 'bus') return 'bus';
  if (partId.startsWith('sol')) return 'sol';
  if (partId.startsWith('ant')) return 'ant';
  return 'sm';
}

export function getDimensionKey(partId) {
  if (partId === 'tank') return 'tank';
  if (DATA.engines[partId]) return partId === 'ion' ? 'ion' : 'eng';
  if (partId.startsWith('bus')) return 'bus';
  return partId;
}

export function getAssetFilename(partId) {
  if (partId === 'tank') return 'part_tank';
  if (DATA.engines[partId]) return DATA.engines[partId].img;
  return partId;
}

export function saveUndoSnapshot() {
  appState.undoHistory.push(JSON.stringify([appState.mission.parts, appState.mission.nu]));
  if (appState.undoHistory.length > MAX_HISTORY) {
    appState.undoHistory.shift();
  }
}

/* ---- Web Audio Synthesizer & Smooth Crossfading Screen Music Controller ---- */
export const AudioEngine = (() => {
  const MAX_MUSIC_VOL = 0.48;
  const MAX_SFX_GAIN = 0.6;
  const CROSSFADE_MS = 700;

  let savedLevel = 0.75;
  try {
    const raw = parseFloat(localStorage[VOLUME_STORAGE_KEY]);
    if (!Number.isNaN(raw) && raw >= 0 && raw <= 1) savedLevel = raw;
  } catch {}

  let ctx = null;
  let masterGain = null;
  let masterLevel = savedLevel;
  let lastNonZeroLevel = savedLevel > 0 ? savedLevel : 0.75;
  let isSoundOn = savedLevel > 0;
  let activeTrackKey = 'landing';
  let activeAudioEl = null;
  let pausedMusicEl = null;
  let unlockBound = false;

  const trackElements = {};
  const fadeTimers = new WeakMap();

  const getMusicTargetVolume = () => (isSoundOn ? MAX_MUSIC_VOL * masterLevel : 0);
  const getSfxTargetGain = () => (isSoundOn ? MAX_SFX_GAIN * masterLevel : 0);

  const persistVolume = () => {
    try {
      localStorage[VOLUME_STORAGE_KEY] = String(masterLevel);
    } catch {}
  };

  const fadeAudio = (audio, targetVol, durationMs, onDone = null) => {
    if (!audio) return;
    const prevRaf = fadeTimers.get(audio);
    if (prevRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(prevRaf);

    const startVol = audio.volume;
    const startTime = performance.now();

    const step = now => {
      const progress = Math.min(1, (now - startTime) / durationMs);
      const eased = progress < 0.5
        ? 2 * progress * progress
        : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * progress + 2, 2) / 2;

      audio.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, startVol + (targetVol - startVol) * eased));

      if (progress < 1) {
        fadeTimers.set(audio, requestAnimationFrame(step));
      } else {
        fadeTimers.delete(audio);
        if (onDone) onDone();
      }
    };

    fadeTimers.set(audio, requestAnimationFrame(step));
  };

  const getTrackElement = key => {
    if (trackElements[key]) return trackElements[key];
    const src = CONTENT.audioTracks?.[key];
    if (!src) return null;

    const audio = new Audio(src);
    audio.preload = 'auto';
    audio.loop = key === 'landing' || key === 'design';
    audio.volume = 0;
    audio.onerror = () => {
      audio.dataset.missing = '1';
    };
    trackElements[key] = audio;
    return audio;
  };

  const fadeOutAndStop = audio => {
    if (!audio) return;
    fadeAudio(audio, 0, CROSSFADE_MS, () => {
      audio.pause();
      audio.currentTime = 0;
    });
  };

  const bindAutoplayUnlock = () => {
    if (unlockBound) return;
    unlockBound = true;

    const unlock = () => {
      init();
      if (isSoundOn && activeTrackKey) {
        const el = getTrackElement(activeTrackKey);
        if (el && el.dataset.missing !== '1' && el.paused) {
          el.volume = 0;
          el.play()
            .then(() => {
              fadeAudio(el, getMusicTargetVolume(), CROSSFADE_MS);
              cleanup();
            })
            .catch(() => {});
        } else if (el && !el.paused) {
          cleanup();
        }
      } else {
        cleanup();
      }
    };

    const cleanup = () => {
      ['pointerdown', 'keydown', 'touchstart'].forEach(evt =>
        document.removeEventListener(evt, unlock, true)
      );
    };

    ['pointerdown', 'keydown', 'touchstart'].forEach(evt =>
      document.addEventListener(evt, unlock, true)
    );
  };

  const playTrack = (key, forceRestart = false) => {
    activeTrackKey = key;
    if (!isSoundOn) return;

    if (!key) {
      if (activeAudioEl) {
        fadeOutAndStop(activeAudioEl);
        activeAudioEl = null;
      }
      return;
    }

    const nextEl = getTrackElement(key);
    if (!nextEl || nextEl.dataset.missing === '1') {
      if (activeAudioEl) {
        fadeOutAndStop(activeAudioEl);
        activeAudioEl = null;
      }
      return;
    }

    if (activeAudioEl === nextEl && !nextEl.paused && !forceRestart) {
      fadeAudio(nextEl, getMusicTargetVolume(), CROSSFADE_MS);
      return;
    }

    if (activeAudioEl && activeAudioEl !== nextEl) {
      fadeOutAndStop(activeAudioEl);
    }

    activeAudioEl = nextEl;
    if (forceRestart) nextEl.currentTime = 0;
    nextEl.volume = 0;
    nextEl.play()
      .then(() => fadeAudio(nextEl, getMusicTargetVolume(), CROSSFADE_MS))
      .catch(() => bindAutoplayUnlock());
  };

  const init = () => {
    if (!ctx) {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (AudioCtx) {
        ctx = new AudioCtx();
        masterGain = ctx.createGain();
        masterGain.gain.value = getSfxTargetGain();
        masterGain.connect(ctx.destination);
      }
    }
    if (ctx && ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  const playTone = (freq, delay, duration, type = 'sine', volume = 0.2, endFreq = null) => {
    if (!isSoundOn || masterLevel <= 0) return;
    init();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    const startTime = ctx.currentTime + delay;

    osc.type = type;
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, startTime);
    if (endFreq) osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(endFreq, startTime + duration);

    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(volume, startTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, startTime + duration);

    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(masterGain);
    osc.start(startTime);
    osc.stop(startTime + duration);
  };

  return {
    init,
    isEnabled: () => isSoundOn,
    getVolume: () => Math.round(masterLevel * 100),
    setVolume(percent) {
      const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(percent) || 0)) / 100;
      masterLevel = clamped;
      if (clamped > 0) {
        lastNonZeroLevel = clamped;
        isSoundOn = true;
      } else {
        isSoundOn = false;
      }
      persistVolume();
      init();
      if (masterGain) masterGain.gain.value = getSfxTargetGain();

      if (isSoundOn) {
        if (activeAudioEl && !activeAudioEl.paused) {
          const prevRaf = fadeTimers.get(activeAudioEl);
          if (prevRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(prevRaf);
          activeAudioEl.volume = getMusicTargetVolume();
        } else {
          playTrack(activeTrackKey || 'landing');
        }
      } else if (activeAudioEl) {
        fadeOutAndStop(activeAudioEl);
        activeAudioEl = null;
      }
      return { isSoundOn, volumePct: Math.round(masterLevel * 100) };
    },
    setMusic: (key, forceRestart = false) => playTrack(key, forceRestart),
    // Flight pause: freeze sound effects and any playing music track.
    pauseAll() {
      if (ctx && ctx.state === 'running') ctx.suspend().catch(() => {});
      if (activeAudioEl && !activeAudioEl.paused) {
        pausedMusicEl = activeAudioEl;
        activeAudioEl.pause();
      }
    },
    // Resume after a pause. Pass false to forget the paused track instead of playing it (flight aborted).
    resumeAll(playMusic = true) {
      if (ctx && ctx.state === 'suspended') ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
      if (pausedMusicEl && playMusic && pausedMusicEl === activeAudioEl) pausedMusicEl.play().catch(() => {});
      pausedMusicEl = null;
    },
    toggleMute() {
      isSoundOn = !isSoundOn;
      if (isSoundOn && masterLevel <= 0) {
        masterLevel = lastNonZeroLevel;
      }
      persistVolume();
      init();
      if (masterGain) masterGain.gain.value = getSfxTargetGain();
      if (isSoundOn) {
        playTrack(activeTrackKey || 'landing');
      } else if (activeAudioEl) {
        fadeOutAndStop(activeAudioEl);
        activeAudioEl = null;
      }
      return { isSoundOn, volumePct: Math.round(masterLevel * 100) };
    },
    click: () => playTone(660, 0, 0.05, 'square', 0.06),
    attach: () => {
      playTone(520, 0, 0.08, 'triangle', 0.14);
      playTone(780, 0.07, 0.1, 'triangle', 0.11);
    },
    remove: () => playTone(420, 0, 0.12, 'triangle', 0.12, 240),
    warn: () => {
      playTone(310, 0, 0.14, 'sawtooth', 0.07);
      playTone(310, 0.2, 0.14, 'sawtooth', 0.07);
    },
    tick: () => playTone(900, 0, 0.04, 'square', 0.05),
    launch: () => playTone(65, 0, 2.5, 'sawtooth', 0.14, 110),
    win: () => {
      playTrack('win', true);
      [523, 659, 784, 1047].forEach((f, i) => playTone(f, i * 0.14, 0.45, 'triangle', 0.16));
    },
    partial: () => {
      // Partial success is not a win: short two-note chime only, no victory track.
      [523, 659].forEach((f, i) => playTone(f, i * 0.2, 0.45, 'triangle', 0.14));
    },
    fail: () => {
      playTrack('loss', true);
      playTone(120, 0, 1.0, 'sawtooth', 0.22, 35);
    }
  };
})();

--- FILE: ./js/main.js ---
/* File: js/main.js
 * Owner: Person B (Saurav)
 * AI-assisted: YES — Gemini, 2026-11-15. Reviewed by a human: YES Reviewd by Person C (Yochan)
 * Sources used: none
 */
import { CONTENT } from './content.js';
import { fetchMissionReferenceData } from './api/nasaService.js';
import {
  appState,
  createInitialMission,
  loadSavedMissionProgress,
  AudioEngine
} from './core/state.js';
import { buildStarterSpacecraft } from './core/engine.js';
import { $ } from './ui/domElements.js';
import { navigateToStage, renderHelpModal } from './ui/renderer.js';
import { bindGlobalControls } from './ui/controls.js';

function initBackgroundVideo() {
  const videoEl = $('#bg');
  if (!videoEl) return;
  if (matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
    videoEl.remove();
    return;
  }
  videoEl.onerror = () => videoEl.remove();
  videoEl.src = CONTENT.bgVideo;
  videoEl.play().catch(() => {});
}

async function initApp() {
  initBackgroundVideo();

  $('#cr').textContent = CONTENT.bgCredit;
  $('#title-disclaimer').textContent = CONTENT.disclaimer;
  document.title = `${CONTENT.appName} — Space Mission Design Game`;
  $('#hn').textContent = CONTENT.appName;
  $('#tt').textContent = CONTENT.appName;
  $('#tg').textContent = CONTENT.tagline;
  renderHelpModal();

  const restored = loadSavedMissionProgress();
  if (!restored) {
    appState.mission = createInitialMission(1);
    buildStarterSpacecraft();
  }

  bindGlobalControls();
  AudioEngine.setMusic('landing');
  navigateToStage(1);

  try {
    const serviceResult = await fetchMissionReferenceData();
    appState.dataSourceLabel = serviceResult.sourceMode;
    appState.spaceWeather = serviceResult.spaceWeather;
    if (appState.mission.stage === 1) {
      navigateToStage(1);
    }
  } catch {}
}

initApp();

--- FILE: ./js/ui/controls.js ---
/* File: js/ui/controls.js
 * Owner: Person B (Saurav)
 * AI-assisted (update): Claude, 2026-11-15 - centred flight menu (M opens it; Home / Pause P / Resume X / Restart R). Reviewed by a human: NO.
 * AI-assisted (update): Claude, 2026-11-15 - win/loss tightening (outcome bands, audio, demos). Reviewed by a human: NO.
 * AI-assisted: YES — Gemini, 2026-11-15. Reviewed by a human: YES Reviewd by Person C (Yochan)
 * Sources used: none
 */
import { DATA } from '../api/mockData.js';
import { isContractOpen, isDeveloperMode, toggleDeveloperMode, resetCareer } from '../core/career.js';
import { ATTACHMENT_NODES, MAX_PARTS, SNAP_RADIUS_PX } from '../config.js';
import {
  appState,
  getNodeCategory,
  saveUndoSnapshot,
  saveMissionProgress,
  saveNamedBuild,
  deleteSavedBuild,
  AudioEngine
} from '../core/state.js';
import {
  findPartByUid,
  getNodeWorldPosition,
  getSubtreeUids,
  getOccupiedNodes,
  createPartInstance,
  deletePartSubtree,
  attachParts,
  mirrorPartIfEnabled,
  autoAttachPart,
  buildStarterSpacecraft,
  buildFailingDemoSpacecraft,
  buildDeveloperPrebuilt,
  isStackNode
} from '../core/engine.js';
import { $, showToast } from './domElements.js';
import {
  navigateToStage,
  renderObjectiveStage,
  refreshBuilderView,
  centerBuilderCanvas,
  autoFitBuilderCanvas,
  updateBuilderLiveDrag,
  updateCareerButton,
  showSavedBuildsModal,
  showReviewModal,
  showCompareModal,
  showDesignCodeModal,
  showCareerModal,
  startLaunchSequence,
  abortLaunchSequence,
  pauseFlight,
  resumeFlight,
  isFlightPaused,
  restartLaunchSequence,
  openFlightMenu,
  closeFlightMenu
} from './renderer.js';

const DEVELOPER_CHEAT_CODE = 'Eventhorizon';

function clientToWorldCoords(event) {
  const rect = $('#world').getBoundingClientRect();
  return [
    (event.clientX - rect.left) / appState.canvasZoom,
    (event.clientY - rect.top) / appState.canvasZoom
  ];
}

function rotateSelectedPart(direction) {
  if (appState.mission.locked) return showToast('Design is locked. Click "Design locked: ON" to unlock.');
  const part = appState.selectedPartUid && findPartByUid(appState.selectedPartUid);
  if (!part) return;

  saveUndoSnapshot();
  const angle = direction * 90;
  const rad = (angle * Math.PI) / 180;
  const cos = Math.cos(rad);
  const sin = Math.sin(rad);
  const pivot = part.par != null ? getNodeWorldPosition(part, part.cn) : [part.x, part.y];

  getSubtreeUids(part.u).forEach(uid => {
    const q = findPartByUid(uid);
    const relX = q.x - pivot[0];
    const relY = q.y - pivot[1];
    q.x = pivot[0] + relX * cos - relY * sin;
    q.y = pivot[1] + relX * sin + relY * cos;
    q.rot = (q.rot + angle + 360) % 360;
  });

  AudioEngine.click();
  refreshBuilderView();
}

function flipSelectedPart() {
  if (appState.mission.locked) return showToast('Design is locked. Click "Design locked: ON" to unlock.');
  const part = appState.selectedPartUid && findPartByUid(appState.selectedPartUid);
  if (!part) return;

  saveUndoSnapshot();
  const pivotX = part.par != null ? getNodeWorldPosition(part, part.cn)[0] : part.x;

  getSubtreeUids(part.u).forEach(uid => {
    const q = findPartByUid(uid);
    q.x = 2 * pivotX - q.x;
    q.flip ^= 1;
    q.rot = (360 - q.rot) % 360;
  });

  AudioEngine.click();
  refreshBuilderView();
}

function deleteSelectedPart() {
  if (appState.mission.locked) return showToast('Design is locked. Click "Design locked: ON" to unlock.');
  const part = appState.selectedPartUid && findPartByUid(appState.selectedPartUid);
  if (!part) return;

  saveUndoSnapshot();
  deletePartSubtree(part.u);
  appState.selectedPartUid = null;
  AudioEngine.remove();
  refreshBuilderView();
}

function undoLastAction() {
  if (appState.mission.locked) return showToast('Design is locked. Click "Design locked: ON" to unlock.');
  const snapshot = appState.undoHistory.pop();
  if (!snapshot) return showToast('Nothing to undo.');

  [appState.mission.parts, appState.mission.nu] = JSON.parse(snapshot);
  appState.selectedPartUid = null;
  refreshBuilderView();
}

function activateDeveloperMode() {
  const enabled = toggleDeveloperMode();
  updateCareerButton();
  if (appState.mission.stage === 1) renderObjectiveStage();
  showToast(
    enabled
      ? 'Developer Mode active: all destinations & 3★ prebuilt rockets unlocked in My builds.'
      : 'Developer Mode off: standard Career locks restored.'
  );
}

function closeOpenModals() {
  $('#rev').hidden = true;
  $('#help').hidden = true;
  closeFlightMenu();
}

// Leaves the flight (if any) and shows the home / title screen.
function goHome() {
  abortLaunchSequence();
  closeOpenModals();
  AudioEngine.click();
  $('#title').hidden = false;
  AudioEngine.setMusic('landing');
}

function syncAudioControls({ isSoundOn, volumePct }) {
  const muteBtn = $('#mute');
  const volSlider = $('#vol');
  const wrapper = muteBtn?.closest('.audio-control');

  if (muteBtn) {
    muteBtn.textContent = `Sound: ${isSoundOn ? 'on' : 'off'}`;
    muteBtn.classList.toggle('sound-on', isSoundOn);
  }
  if (volSlider) {
    volSlider.value = isSoundOn ? String(volumePct) : '0';
    volSlider.title = `Master volume (${isSoundOn ? volumePct : 0}%)`;
  }
  if (wrapper) {
    wrapper.classList.toggle('is-muted', !isSoundOn);
  }
}

export function bindGlobalControls() {
  syncAudioControls({
    isSoundOn: AudioEngine.isEnabled(),
    volumePct: AudioEngine.getVolume()
  });

  document.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {
    e.preventDefault();
  });

  $('#brand-home')?.addEventListener('click', e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    goHome();
  });

  // Flight menu: the header Menu button opens a centred panel with Home / Pause / Resume / Restart
  const flightMenu = $('#flightmenu');
  $('#fm-open')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    AudioEngine.click();
    openFlightMenu();
  });
  $('#fm-close')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    AudioEngine.click();
    closeFlightMenu();
  });
  $('#fm-home')?.addEventListener('click', goHome);
  $('#fm-pause')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    AudioEngine.click();
    pauseFlight();
  });
  $('#fm-resume')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    AudioEngine.click();
    resumeFlight();
  });
  $('#fm-restart')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    AudioEngine.click();
    restartLaunchSequence();
  });
  // Clicking the dark area around the panel closes the menu
  flightMenu?.addEventListener('click', e => {
    if (e.target === flightMenu) closeFlightMenu();
  });
  // Keep Tab inside the open menu
  flightMenu?.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key !== 'Tab') return;
    const items = [...flightMenu.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled)')];
    if (!items.length) return;
    const first = items[0];
    const last = items[items.length - 1];
    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
      e.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
      e.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    } else if (!items.includes(document.activeElement)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  });

  // Flight shortcuts: Backspace = home, M = menu, P = pause, X = resume, R = restart (only while a launch is running)
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.repeat || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
    const tag = e.target.tagName;
    if (/SELECT|TEXTAREA/.test(tag) || (tag === 'INPUT' && e.target.type !== 'range')) return;
    const key = (e.key || '').toLowerCase();

    // M opens or closes the flight menu whenever the header Menu button is showing
    if (key === 'm' && !$('#fm-open').hidden) {
      e.preventDefault();
      AudioEngine.click();
      if ($('#flightmenu').hidden) openFlightMenu();
      else closeFlightMenu();
      return;
    }
    // Backspace goes Home from the flight screen (same as the Home button)
    if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !$('#fm-open').hidden) {
      e.preventDefault();
      goHome();
      return;
    }
    if (!appState.isFlying) return;

    if (key === 'p') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (!isFlightPaused()) {
        AudioEngine.click();
        pauseFlight();
      }
    } else if (key === 'x') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (isFlightPaused()) {
        AudioEngine.click();
        resumeFlight();
      }
    } else if (key === 'r') {
      e.preventDefault();
      AudioEngine.click();
      restartLaunchSequence();
    }
  });

  let logoClicks = 0;
  let logoClickTimer = null;
  $('#tt')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    logoClicks++;
    clearTimeout(logoClickTimer);
    logoClickTimer = setTimeout(() => { logoClicks = 0; }, 2200);
    if (logoClicks >= 5) {
      logoClicks = 0;
      activateDeveloperMode();
    }
  });

  // Case-sensitive developer cheat code: exact "Eventhorizon" (only E capital)
  let keyBuffer = '';
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      closeOpenModals();
      return;
    }
    if (/SELECT|TEXTAREA|INPUT/.test(e.target.tagName)) return;
    if (e.key && e.key.length === 1) {
      keyBuffer = (keyBuffer + e.key).slice(-DEVELOPER_CHEAT_CODE.length);
      if (keyBuffer === DEVELOPER_CHEAT_CODE) {
        keyBuffer = '';
        activateDeveloperMode();
      }
    }
  });

  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
    if (appState.mission.stage !== 2) return;

    const placedEl = e.target.closest('.pt[data-u]');
    const paletteEl = e.target.closest('[data-p]');
    if (!placedEl && !paletteEl) return;
    e.preventDefault();

    if (appState.mission.locked) {
      if (placedEl) {
        const part = findPartByUid(+placedEl.dataset.u);
        if (part && appState.selectedPartUid !== part.u) {
          appState.selectedPartUid = part.u;
          AudioEngine.click();
          refreshBuilderView();
        }
      }
      showToast('Design is locked. Click "Design locked: ON" to customize parts.');
      return;
    }

    if (paletteEl) {
      appState.activeDrag = {
        nid: paletteEl.dataset.p,
        pointerId: e.pointerId,
        x: e.clientX,
        y: e.clientY,
        mv: false
      };
      return;
    }

    const part = findPartByUid(+placedEl.dataset.u);
    if (!part) return;

    if (appState.selectedPartUid !== part.u) {
      appState.selectedPartUid = part.u;
      AudioEngine.click();
      refreshBuilderView();
    }

    appState.activeDrag = {
      u: part.u,
      pointerId: e.pointerId,
      w0: clientToWorldCoords(e),
      x: e.clientX,
      y: e.clientY,
      mv: false
    };
  });

  document.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
    const drag = appState.activeDrag;
    if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.pointerId) return;

    if (!drag.mv) {
      if (Math.hypot(e.clientX - drag.x, e.clientY - drag.y) < 7) return;

      if (drag.nid) {
        const partId = drag.nid;
        const hasCategory = cat => appState.mission.parts.some(p => getNodeCategory(p.id) === cat);

        if (hasCategory('bus') && getNodeCategory(partId) === 'bus') {
          appState.activeDrag = null;
          return showToast('One bus per ship. Click a different bus in the list to swap it.');
        }
        if (hasCategory('eng') && getNodeCategory(partId) === 'eng') {
          appState.activeDrag = null;
          return showToast('One main engine. Click a different engine in the list to swap it.');
        }
        if (appState.mission.parts.length >= MAX_PARTS) {
          appState.activeDrag = null;
          return showToast(`Part limit reached (${MAX_PARTS}).`);
        }

        saveUndoSnapshot();
        const worldPos = clientToWorldCoords(e);
        const newPart = createPartInstance(partId, worldPos[0], worldPos[1]);
        appState.selectedPartUid = newPart.u;
        appState.activeDrag = {
          u: newPart.u,
          pointerId: e.pointerId,
          w0: worldPos,
          x: e.clientX,
          y: e.clientY,
          mv: true
        };
        refreshBuilderView();
      } else {
        saveUndoSnapshot();
        const rootPart = findPartByUid(drag.u);
        if (!rootPart) {
          appState.activeDrag = null;
          return;
        }
        rootPart.par = null;
        drag.mv = true;
        refreshBuilderView();
      }

      appState.activeDrag.orig = {};
      getSubtreeUids(appState.activeDrag.u).forEach(uid => {
        const q = findPartByUid(uid);
        appState.activeDrag.orig[uid] = [q.x, q.y];
      });
      AudioEngine.click();
    }

    const activeDrag = appState.activeDrag;
    const worldPos = clientToWorldCoords(e);
    const dx = worldPos[0] - activeDrag.w0[0];
    const dy = worldPos[1] - activeDrag.w0[1];
    const draggedPart = findPartByUid(activeDrag.u);
    if (!draggedPart) return;

    getSubtreeUids(activeDrag.u).forEach(uid => {
      const q = findPartByUid(uid);
      q.x = activeDrag.orig[uid][0] + dx;
      q.y = activeDrag.orig[uid][1] + dy;
    });

    activeDrag.sn = null;
    if (getNodeCategory(draggedPart.id) !== 'bus') {
      const occupied = getOccupiedNodes();
      const draggedTree = new Set(getSubtreeUids(activeDrag.u));
      let closestDist = SNAP_RADIUS_PX;

      const hasChildren = appState.mission.parts.some(q => q.par === draggedPart.u);
      const origFlip = draggedPart.flip;
      const origRot = draggedPart.rot;

      ATTACHMENT_NODES[getNodeCategory(draggedPart.id)].forEach(([childNode]) => {
        if (occupied.has(`${draggedPart.u}:${childNode}`)) return;

        appState.mission.parts.forEach(host => {
          if (draggedTree.has(host.u)) return;

          ATTACHMENT_NODES[getNodeCategory(host.id)].forEach(([hostNode]) => {
            if (occupied.has(`${host.u}:${hostNode}`) || isStackNode(childNode) !== isStackNode(hostNode)) return;

            if (!isStackNode(childNode) && !hasChildren) {
              draggedPart.rot = 0;
              draggedPart.flip = hostNode.startsWith('L') ? 1 : 0;
            }

            const a = getNodeWorldPosition(draggedPart, childNode);
            const b = getNodeWorldPosition(host, hostNode);
            const distance = Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]);

            draggedPart.flip = origFlip;
            draggedPart.rot = origRot;

            if (distance < closestDist) {
              closestDist = distance;
              activeDrag.sn = { h: host.u, hn: hostNode, cn: childNode };
            }
          });
        });
      });

      if (activeDrag.sn) {
        attachParts(draggedPart, activeDrag.sn.cn, findPartByUid(activeDrag.sn.h), activeDrag.sn.hn);
        draggedPart.par = null;
      }
    }

    updateBuilderLiveDrag();
  });

  const finishPointerDrag = e => {
    const drag = appState.activeDrag;
    if (!drag || (e.pointerId !== undefined && e.pointerId !== drag.pointerId)) return;
    appState.activeDrag = null;

    if (!drag.mv) {
      if (drag.nid) {
        const result = autoAttachPart(drag.nid);
        if (!result.ok) showToast(result.message);
        else AudioEngine.attach();
      }
      refreshBuilderView();
      return;
    }

    const paletteRect = $('#pal').getBoundingClientRect();
    const droppedOnPalette =
      e.clientX > paletteRect.left &&
      e.clientX < paletteRect.right &&
      e.clientY > paletteRect.top &&
      e.clientY < paletteRect.bottom;

    if (droppedOnPalette) {
      deletePartSubtree(drag.u);
      appState.selectedPartUid = null;
      AudioEngine.remove();
    } else if (drag.sn) {
      const part = findPartByUid(drag.u);
      attachParts(part, drag.sn.cn, findPartByUid(drag.sn.h), drag.sn.hn);
      mirrorPartIfEnabled(part);
      AudioEngine.attach();
    }

    refreshBuilderView();
  };

  document.addEventListener('pointerup', finishPointerDrag);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel', finishPointerDrag);

  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
      const cardEl = e.target.closest('.card[data-a]');
      if (cardEl) {
        e.preventDefault();
        cardEl.click();
        return;
      }
      const placedPartEl = e.target.closest('.pt[data-u]');
      if (placedPartEl) {
        e.preventDefault();
        appState.selectedPartUid = +placedPartEl.dataset.u;
        refreshBuilderView();
        return;
      }
    }

    if (appState.mission.stage !== 2 || /SELECT|TEXTAREA|INPUT/.test(e.target.tagName)) return;
    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();

    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
      if (key === 'z') {
        e.preventDefault();
        undoLastAction();
      }
      return;
    }

    if (key === 'r') rotateSelectedPart(e.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
    else if (key === 'f') flipSelectedPart();
    else if (key === 'delete' || key === 'backspace') {
      e.preventDefault();
      deleteSelectedPart();
    }
  });

  let panState = null;
  document.addEventListener('wheel', e => {
    if (appState.mission.stage !== 2 || !e.target.closest('#cv')) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const delta = e.deltaY < 0 ? 0.08 : -0.08;
    appState.canvasZoom = Math.max(0.45, Math.min(1.5, +(appState.canvasZoom + delta).toFixed(2)));
    refreshBuilderView();
    centerBuilderCanvas();
  }, { passive: false });

  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
    if (appState.mission.stage !== 2) return;
    const cv = e.target.closest('#cv');
    if (!cv || e.target.closest('.pt[data-u]') || e.target.closest('[data-p]')) return;
    panState = { cv, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, sl: cv.scrollLeft, st: cv.scrollTop };
  });

  document.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
    if (!panState) return;
    panState.cv.scrollLeft = panState.sl - (e.clientX - panState.x);
    panState.cv.scrollTop = panState.st - (e.clientY - panState.y);
  });

  document.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
    panState = null;
  });

  document.addEventListener('click', e => {
    if (e.target.closest('[data-close-modal]') || e.target.id === 'rev' || e.target.id === 'help') {
      AudioEngine.click();
      closeOpenModals();
      return;
    }

    const prebuiltBtn = e.target.closest('[data-load-prebuilt]');
    if (prebuiltBtn) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (!isDeveloperMode()) return showToast('Unlock Developer Mode first to load prebuilt reference rockets.');
      saveUndoSnapshot();
      const loaded = buildDeveloperPrebuilt(prebuiltBtn.dataset.loadPrebuilt);
      if (loaded) {
        appState.selectedPartUid = null;
        saveMissionProgress();
        AudioEngine.attach();
        $('#rev').hidden = true;
        navigateToStage(2);
        autoFitBuilderCanvas();
        showToast(`Loaded 3★ Prebuilt: ${loaded.title}`);
      }
      return;
    }

    const actionEl = e.target.closest('[data-a], [data-st]');
    if (actionEl) {
      AudioEngine.click();
      const { st, a, v } = actionEl.dataset;

      if (st) return navigateToStage(+st);
      if (a === 'ci') {
        if (!isContractOpen(DATA.contracts[+v].id)) return showToast('That destination is locked. Check Career for the requirement.');
        appState.mission.ci = +v;
        appState.mission.oi = 0;
        saveMissionProgress();
        return renderObjectiveStage();
      }
      if (a === 'oi') { appState.mission.oi = +v; saveMissionProgress(); return renderObjectiveStage(); }
      if (a === 'L') { appState.mission.L = v; saveMissionProgress(); return renderObjectiveStage(); }
      if (a === 'hd') { appState.mission.hard = +v; saveMissionProgress(); return renderObjectiveStage(); }
      if (a === 'go2') return navigateToStage(2);
      if (a === 'go1') return navigateToStage(1);
      if (a === 'deb') return navigateToStage(4);
      if (a === 'rot') return rotateSelectedPart(1);
      if (a === 'flp') return flipSelectedPart();
      if (a === 'del') return deleteSelectedPart();
      if (a === 'undo') return undoLastAction();
      if (a === 'zi') {
        appState.canvasZoom = Math.min(1.3, +(appState.canvasZoom + 0.1).toFixed(2));
        refreshBuilderView();
        centerBuilderCanvas();
        return;
      }
      if (a === 'zo') {
        appState.canvasZoom = Math.max(0.45, +(appState.canvasZoom - 0.1).toFixed(2));
        refreshBuilderView();
        centerBuilderCanvas();
        return;
      }
      if (a === 'rev') return showReviewModal();
      return;
    }

    if (e.target.id === 'rgo') {
      $('#rev').hidden = true;
      startLaunchSequence();
    }

    if (e.target.id === 'car') showCareerModal();

    if (e.target.id === 'creset') {
      resetCareer();
      localStorage.removeItem('apogeeMissionState');
      localStorage.removeItem('apogeeSavedBuilds');
      location.reload();
    }

    if (e.target.id === 'confirm-save-build') {
      const inputEl = $('#new-build-name');
      const res = saveNamedBuild(inputEl ? inputEl.value : '');
      if (!res.ok) return showToast(res.message);
      AudioEngine.attach();
      const myBuildsBtn = $('#my-builds');
      if (myBuildsBtn) myBuildsBtn.textContent = `My builds (${appState.savedBuilds.length})`;
      showSavedBuildsModal();
      showToast(`Saved "${res.entry.name}".`);
    }

    const loadBuildBtn = e.target.closest('[data-load-build]');
    const delBuildBtn = e.target.closest('[data-del-build]');
    if (loadBuildBtn) {
      const build = appState.savedBuilds.find(b => b.id === loadBuildBtn.dataset.loadBuild);
      if (build) {
        if (DATA.contracts[build.mission.ci] && !isContractOpen(DATA.contracts[build.mission.ci].id)) {
          return showToast('That saved build targets a destination still locked in Career mode.');
        }
        saveUndoSnapshot();
        appState.mission = { ...appState.mission, ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(build.mission)), stage: 2, locked: false };
        appState.selectedPartUid = null;
        saveMissionProgress();
        $('#rev').hidden = true;
        navigateToStage(2);
        showToast(`Loaded "${build.name}".`);
      }
    }
    if (delBuildBtn) {
      deleteSavedBuild(delBuildBtn.dataset.delBuild);
      const myBuildsBtn = $('#my-builds');
      if (myBuildsBtn) myBuildsBtn.textContent = `My builds (${appState.savedBuilds.length})`;
      showSavedBuildsModal();
    }

    const loadCandidateBtn = e.target.closest('[data-c]');
    const deleteCandidateBtn = e.target.closest('[data-x]');
    if (loadCandidateBtn) {
      const candidateData = JSON.parse(appState.candidates[+loadCandidateBtn.dataset.c]);
      saveUndoSnapshot();
      appState.mission = { ...appState.mission, ...candidateData, stage: 2, locked: false };
      appState.selectedPartUid = null;
      saveMissionProgress();
      $('#rev').hidden = true;
      navigateToStage(2);
    }
    if (deleteCandidateBtn) {
      appState.candidates.splice(+deleteCandidateBtn.dataset.x, 1);
      saveMissionProgress();
      appState.candidates.length ? showCompareModal() : ($('#rev').hidden = true);
    }

    if (e.target.id === 'start') {
      $('#title').hidden = true;
      AudioEngine.setMusic('design');
      navigateToStage(1);
    }

    if (e.target.id === 'demo-ok') {
      $('#title').hidden = true;
      appState.mission.locked = false;
      appState.mission.ci = 1; // Moon, so the demo always meets its goal
      appState.mission.oi = 0;
      appState.mission.L = 'medium';
      appState.mission.hard = 0;
      buildStarterSpacecraft();
      autoAttachPart('i_spec');
      autoAttachPart('ant_mg');
      autoAttachPart('rec');
      saveMissionProgress();
      AudioEngine.setMusic('design');
      navigateToStage(2);
      showToast('Loaded a complete Moon spacecraft that meets the science goal. Click "Design review and launch" to fly it.');
    }

    if (e.target.id === 'demo-fail') {
      $('#title').hidden = true;
      appState.mission.locked = false;
      buildFailingDemoSpacecraft();
      saveMissionProgress();
      AudioEngine.setMusic('design');
      navigateToStage(2);
      showToast('Loaded an under-fueled Mars design. Launch it to see why it fails.');
    }

    if (['hp', 'hp2'].includes(e.target.id)) {
      $('#help').hidden = false;
    }

    if (e.target.id === 'copy-code-btn') {
      const textArea = $('#design-code-input');
      textArea.select();
      try {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(textArea.value);
        showToast('Design code copied to clipboard.');
      } catch {
        showToast('Press Ctrl+C to copy the highlighted design code.');
      }
    }

    if (e.target.id === 'load-code-btn') {
      try {
        const raw = $('#design-code-input').value.trim();
        const decoded = JSON.parse(atob(raw));
        if (DATA.contracts[decoded.ci] && !isContractOpen(DATA.contracts[decoded.ci].id)) {
          return showToast('That design flies to a destination that is still locked in Career mode.');
        }
        const isValid =
          DATA.contracts[decoded.ci] &&
          DATA.launchers[decoded.L] &&
          Array.isArray(decoded.parts) &&
          decoded.parts.every(p => p.id === 'tank' || DATA.parts[p.id] || DATA.engines[p.id]);

        if (isValid) {
          appState.mission = { ...appState.mission, ...decoded, stage: 2, locked: false };
          saveMissionProgress();
          $('#rev').hidden = true;
          navigateToStage(2);
          showToast('Design loaded successfully.');
        } else {
          showToast('Invalid design code structure.');
        }
      } catch {
        showToast('Could not decode design string.');
      }
    }
  });

  $('#mute').addEventListener('click', () => {
    syncAudioControls(AudioEngine.toggleMute());
  });

  $('#vol')?.addEventListener('input', e => {
    syncAudioControls(AudioEngine.setVolume(e.target.value));
  });

  $('#copy').addEventListener('click', () => {
    showDesignCodeModal();
  });
}

export function showToast(message) {
  const toastEl = $('#toast');
  toastEl.textContent = message;
  toastEl.hidden = false;
  clearTimeout(showToast.timerId);
  showToast.timerId = setTimeout(() => {
    toastEl.hidden = true;
  }, 3000);
}

const WORLD_PAD_PX = 360;
let activeFlightCleanup = null;
let cancelActiveBurn = null;
let cancelBurnHold = null;
let flightRunId = 0;

/* ---- Flight clock: every launch animation, burn timer and log delay reads this clock,
   so freezing it pauses the whole flight at once. ---- */
const flightClock = {
  pausedAt: null,
  offset: 0,
  now() {
    return (this.pausedAt ?? performance.now()) - this.offset;
  },
  pause() {
    if (this.pausedAt === null) this.pausedAt = performance.now();
  },
  resume() {
    if (this.pausedAt === null) return;
    this.offset += performance.now() - this.pausedAt;
    this.pausedAt = null;
  },
  get paused() {
    return this.pausedAt !== null;
  }
};

// Like a plain timeout, but the countdown stops while the flight is paused.
const flightDelay = ms => new Promise(resolve => {
  const startedAt = flightClock.now();
  const check = () => (flightClock.now() - startedAt >= ms ? resolve() : setTimeout(check, 40));
  setTimeout(check, Math.min(ms, 40));
});

export function abortLaunchSequence() {
  if (activeFlightCleanup) {
    activeFlightCleanup();
    activeFlightCleanup = null;
  }
  if (cancelActiveBurn) cancelActiveBurn();
  flightClock.resume();
  AudioEngine.resumeAll(false);
  $('#flv')?.classList.remove('paused');
  const flightMenu = $('#flightmenu');
  if (flightMenu) flightMenu.hidden = true;
  appState.isFlying = false;
  const burnPanel = $('#burn');
  if (burnPanel) {
    burnPanel.hidden = true;
    burnPanel.inert = false;
  }
  $('#nav')?.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn => {
    btn.disabled = btn.dataset.st === '3' || (btn.dataset.st === '4' && !appState.mission.res);
  });
  syncFlightMenu();
}

/* ---- Flight menu: a centred pause menu (Home / Pause / Resume / Restart) ---- */

const isFlightMenuOpen = () => {
  const menu = $('#flightmenu');
  return !!menu && !menu.hidden;
};

// Keeps the header Menu button, the centred menu and the status badge up to date.
export function syncFlightMenu() {
  const menu = $('#flightmenu');
  const opener = $('#fm-open');
  if (!menu || !opener) return;
  const text = CONTENT.flightHud;
  const flying = appState.isFlying;
  const paused = flightClock.paused;
  const available = appState.mission.stage === 3 && (flying || !!appState.mission.res);

  opener.hidden = !available;
  opener.textContent = text.menuButton;
  opener.title = `${text.menuButton} (${text.keyMenu})`;
  if (!available) menu.hidden = true;

  $('#fm-label').textContent = text.menuLabel;
  const setBtn = (id, name, key) => {
    const btn = $(id);
    btn.querySelector('.fm-name').textContent = name;
    const kbd = btn.querySelector('kbd');
    if (kbd) kbd.textContent = key;
  };
  setBtn('#fm-home', text.home, text.keyHome);
  setBtn('#fm-pause', text.pause, text.keyPause);
  setBtn('#fm-resume', text.resume, text.keyResume);
  setBtn('#fm-restart', text.restart, text.keyRestart);
  $('#fm-close').textContent = text.close;

  const status = $('#fm-status');
  status.textContent = !flying ? text.statusComplete : paused ? text.statusPaused : text.statusFlying;
  status.classList.toggle('paused', flying && paused);
  status.classList.toggle('done', !flying);

  $('#fm-pause').disabled = !flying || paused;
  $('#fm-resume').disabled = !flying || !paused;
  $('#fm-restart').disabled = !flying;
}

export function openFlightMenu() {
  const menu = $('#flightmenu');
  if (!menu || $('#fm-open').hidden) return;
  menu.hidden = false;
  syncFlightMenu();
  const first = [$('#fm-resume'), $('#fm-pause'), $('#fm-home')].find(btn => btn && !btn.disabled);
  first?.focus();
}

export function closeFlightMenu() {
  const menu = $('#flightmenu');
  if (!menu || menu.hidden) return;
  menu.hidden = true;
  const opener = $('#fm-open');
  if (opener && !opener.hidden) opener.focus();
}

export const isFlightPaused = () => flightClock.paused;

export function pauseFlight() {
  if (!appState.isFlying || flightClock.paused) return;
  flightClock.pause();
  if (cancelBurnHold) cancelBurnHold(); // a burn you were holding starts over after Resume
  AudioEngine.pauseAll();
  $('#flv')?.classList.add('paused');
  const burnPanel = $('#burn');
  if (burnPanel) burnPanel.inert = true;
  syncFlightMenu();
  if (isFlightMenuOpen()) $('#fm-resume')?.focus();
}

export function resumeFlight() {
  if (!flightClock.paused) return;
  flightClock.resume();
  AudioEngine.resumeAll(true);
  $('#flv')?.classList.remove('paused');
  const burnPanel = $('#burn');
  if (burnPanel) {
    burnPanel.inert = false;
    if (!burnPanel.hidden) $('#burnbtn')?.focus();
  }
  syncFlightMenu();
  if (isFlightMenuOpen()) {
    $('#flightmenu').hidden = true; // Resume puts the player straight back in the flight
    if (document.activeElement?.closest?.('#flightmenu')) document.activeElement.blur();
  }
}

// Throws away the current flight and flies the same design again from lift-off.
export function restartLaunchSequence() {
  if (!appState.isFlying) return;
  abortLaunchSequence();
  showToast(CONTENT.flightHud.restarted);
  startLaunchSequence();
}

const renderDataBadge = isVerified =>
  isVerified
    ? ''
    : '<span class="badge-simplified" title="Simplified engineering model parameter for gameplay">Sim Model</span>';

const renderModalHeader = (titleText, titleId = '') => `
  <div class="modal-header">
    <h2 ${titleId ? `id="${titleId}"` : ''}>${titleText}</h2>
    <button type="button" class="modal-close-x" data-close-modal="1" aria-label="Close window">&times;</button>
  </div>
`;

export function renderHelpModal() {
  const paragraphs = CONTENT.helpHtml
    .trim()
    .split('\n')
    .map(line => line.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map(pHtml => `<div class="help-card">${pHtml}</div>`)
    .join('');

  $('#help-body').innerHTML = `
    ${renderModalHeader('Mission Control Field Guide', 'help-title')}
    <div class="help-grid">${paragraphs}</div>
  `;
}

/* ---- Stage Navigation ---- */

export function navigateToStage(stageNumber) {
  if (appState.isFlying) return;
  appState.mission.stage = stageNumber;
  saveMissionProgress();

  const isTitleVisible = $('#title') && !$('#title').hidden;
  if (isTitleVisible) {
    AudioEngine.setMusic('landing');
  } else if (stageNumber === 1 || stageNumber === 2) {
    AudioEngine.setMusic('design');
  }

  $$('.st').forEach((section, index) => {
    section.classList.toggle('on', index + 1 === stageNumber);
  });

  $('#nav').innerHTML = CONTENT.stageNames.map((label, index) => {
    const num = index + 1;
    const isActive = num === stageNumber;
    const isCompleted = num < stageNumber;
    const isDisabled = num === 3 || (num === 4 && !appState.mission.res);
    return `<button type="button" data-st="${num}" class="${isActive ? 'on' : isCompleted ? 'done' : ''}" ${isDisabled ? 'disabled' : ''}>${label}</button>`;
  }).join('');

  if (stageNumber === 1) renderObjectiveStage();
  if (stageNumber === 2) renderBuilderStage();
  if (stageNumber === 4) renderDebriefStage();

  updateCareerButton();
  syncFlightMenu();
}

export function updateCareerButton() {
  $('#car').textContent = `★ ${getCareerButtonLabel()}`;
}

/* ---- Stage 1: Objective Selection ---- */

const formatOrbitSummary = orbit =>
  `${toPercent(orbit.ecl)}% shadow · ${(orbit.contact * 24).toFixed(0)} h/day contact · detail x${orbit.res} · needs ${orbit.dv} km/s`;

export function renderObjectiveStage() {
  const mission = appState.mission;
  const contract = DATA.contracts[mission.ci];
  const weather = appState.spaceWeather;
  const devMode = isDeveloperMode();

  const contractCards = DATA.contracts
    .map((c, index) => [c, index])
    .sort((a, b) => getContractSortKey(a[0]) - getContractSortKey(b[0]))
    .map(([c, index]) => {
      const open = isContractOpen(c.id);
      const feasibility = getFeasibility(c);
      const lock = open ? null : getLockInfo(c);
      const devPrebuilt = devMode ? DEVELOPER_PREBUILTS.find(p => p.contractId === c.id) : null;
      return `
    <div class="card ${index === mission.ci ? 'sel' : ''} ${open ? '' : 'lkd'}" role="button" ${open ? `tabindex="0" data-a="ci" data-v="${index}"` : 'aria-disabled="true"'}>
      <img src="assets/dest_${c.dest}.svg" alt="${c.n}">
      <div>
        <b>${c.n}</b>
        <p>${CONTENT.contractBriefs[c.id]}</p>
        <small>Baseline science ${c.target} (threshold ${c.thr}) · budget $${c.cap}M · ${c.months} months${appState.highScores[c.id] ? ' · best ' + appState.highScores[c.id] : ''}</small>
        <span class="fb ${feasibility.cls}">${feasibility.lab}</span><small class="fn">${feasibility.notes.join(' · ')}</small>
        ${lock ? `<span class="lk">LOCKED - Level ${lock.level}: ${lock.need}</span>` : ''}
        ${devPrebuilt ? `<div style="margin-top:8px;"><button type="button" data-load-prebuilt="${devPrebuilt.id}" style="min-height:34px; padding:4px 10px; font-size:0.82rem; border-color:var(--ok); color:var(--ok);">Load 3&#9733; Prebuilt &rarr;</button></div>` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
    }).join('');

  const orbitCards = contract.orbits.map((o, index) => `
    <div class="card o ${index === mission.oi ? 'sel' : ''}" role="button" tabindex="0" data-a="oi" data-v="${index}">
      <div>
        <b>${o.n}</b> — ${CONTENT.orbitDescriptions[o.n]}<br>
        <small>${formatOrbitSummary(o)}</small>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');

  const launcherCards = Object.keys(DATA.launchers).map(key => {
    const l = DATA.launchers[key];
    return `
      <div class="card ${mission.L === key ? 'sel' : ''}" role="button" tabindex="0" data-a="L" data-v="${key}">
        <img src="assets/${l.img}.svg" alt="${l.n}">
        <div>
          <b>${l.n}</b>
          <p>Lifts ${formatNumber(l.cap)} kg</p>
          <small>$${l.c}M · ${toPercent(l.rel)}% reliable</small>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');

  $('#s1').innerHTML = `
    <div>
      <h2>1. Choose your mission objective <span class="badge-source ${weather.isLive ? '' : 'offline'}">${appState.dataSourceLabel}</span>${devMode ? ' <span class="badge-simplified">Developer Mode</span>' : ''}</h2>
      <div class="weather-banner ${weather.isLive ? '' : 'offline'}">
        <span><b>Space Weather Telemetry:</b> ${weather.summary}</span>
